===== frontend\README.md =====
# PII/PHI Redaction Frontend

Modern, professional UI for the PII/PHI Redaction Service built with Next.js 14, TypeScript, and Tailwind CSS.

## 🚀 Features

- **Modern UI/UX**: Clean, responsive design with Tailwind CSS
- **File Upload**: Drag-and-drop interface for PDF documents
- **Real-time Processing**: Live progress updates during redaction
- **PDF Viewer**: Inline PDF viewing for redacted documents
- **Review Dashboard**: Comprehensive review and approval workflow
- **Redaction History**: Complete audit trail of all operations
- **Responsive Design**: Works on desktop, tablet, and mobile
- **Authentication**: Secure login system

## 📋 Prerequisites

- Node.js 20+ and npm/yarn
- Python 3.12+ (for backend)
- Running Python backend API (see backend setup)

## 🛠️ Installation

### 1. Install Dependencies

```bash
cd frontend
npm install
```

### 2. Set Up Environment Variables

Create a `.env.local` file:

```bash
NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=http://localhost:8000
```

### 3. Run Development Server

```bash
npm run dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) in your browser.

## 🐍 Python Backend Setup

The Next.js app connects to a FastAPI backend for PII/PHI redaction processing.

### 1. Install Python Dependencies

```bash
cd ..  # Go back to root directory
pip install fastapi uvicorn python-multipart
```

### 2. Run the FastAPI Server

```bash
python api_server.py
```

The API will be available at [http://localhost:8000](http://localhost:8000)

## 📁 Project Structure

```
frontend/
├── app/                    # Next.js app directory
│   ├── globals.css        # Global styles
│   ├── layout.tsx         # Root layout
│   └── page.tsx           # Home page
├── components/            # React components
│   ├── ui/               # Reusable UI components
│   │   ├── button.tsx
│   │   ├── card.tsx
│   │   └── tabs.tsx
│   ├── pii-redaction.tsx  # Main redaction interface
│   ├── pii-review-table.tsx # Review dashboard
│   ├── login.tsx         # Authentication
│   └── overview.tsx      # Overview page
├── context/             # React context
│   └── AnalysisContext.tsx # App state management
├── lib/                 # Utility functions
│   └── utils.ts
├── public/             # Static assets
└── package.json
```

## 🎨 Customization

### Styling

The app uses Tailwind CSS with shadcn/ui components for a consistent, professional design.

### Colors

The app maintains the existing color scheme for consistency. Key colors:
- Primary: `#58bdcc` (cyan)
- Secondary: `#93d500` (green)
- Accent: `#088c87` (dark cyan)

### Authentication

The current authentication is a simple username/password check. Replace the logic in `components/login.tsx` with your actual authentication system.

## 🔧 Building for Production

```bash
npm run build
npm start
```

## 📝 Environment Variables

| Variable | Description | Default |
|----------|-------------|---------|
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` | Backend API URL | `http://localhost:8000` |

## 🔗 API Endpoints

The Next.js app expects these API endpoints:

- `POST /api/pii-redaction/redact` - Redact PII/PHI from documents
- `GET /api/pii-redaction/list` - List redaction records
- `GET /api/pii-redaction/{id}` - Get redaction details
- `GET /api/pii-redaction/{id}/download` - Download redacted document
- `GET /api/health` - Health check

See `../backend/api_server.py` for implementation details.

## 📄 License

Copyright © 2025 Guidehouse. All rights reserved.

===== .github\workflows\deploy-to-azure-dev.yml =====
name: Deploy to Azure - DEV

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
      - dev
permissions:
  id-token: write
  contents: write

env:
  # Use docker.io for Docker Hub if empty
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  # github.repository as <account>/<repo>
jobs:

  build:

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v3

      # Use Cache action to cache docker builds
      - name: Cache Docker layers
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: /tmp/.buildx-cache
          key: ${{ runner.os }}-buildx-${{ github.sha }}
          restore-keys: |
            ${{ runner.os }}-buildx-
            # Set up BuildKit Docker container builder to be able to build
      # multi-platform images and export cache
      # https://github.com/docker/setup-buildx-action
      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@f95db51fddba0c2d1ec667646a06c2ce06100226 # v3.0.0
      # Login against a Docker registry except on PR
      # https://github.com/docker/login-action
      - name: Log into registry ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'
        uses: docker/login-action@343f7c4344506bcbf9b4de18042ae17996df046d # v3.0.0
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}


      - name: Fetch org name
        id: fetch-org-name
        run: |
          echo "ORG_NAME=${{ github.repository_owner }}" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify repo name
        id: modify-repo-name
        run: |
          repository="${{ github.repository }}"
          # Remove 'gh-inner/' prefix
          repository="${repository#gh-inner/}"
          # Replace underscores and spaces with hyphens
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | sed -e 's/[ _]/-/g')
          # Convert to lowercase
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          # Remove any trailing hyphens
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | sed -e 's/-$//')
          # Set the modified repository name as an environment variable
          echo "REPO_NAME=$repository" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify ref name
        id: modify-ref-name
        run: |
          refname=$(echo "${{ github.ref_name }}" | tr "_" "-")
          refname=$(echo "$refname" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          echo "REF_NAME=$refname" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify head ref
        id: modify-head-ref
        run: |
          headref=$(echo "${{ github.head_ref }}" | tr "_" "-")
          headref=$(echo "$headref" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          echo "HEAD_REF=$headref" >> $GITHUB_ENV
          
      - name: extract-config
        id: extract-config
        uses: mikefarah/yq@master
        with:
          cmd: 'yq -o=json eval .guidehouse.yaml'
      # Extract metadata (tags, labels) for Docker
      # https://github.com/docker/metadata-action
      - name: Extract Docker metadata
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@96383f45573cb7f253c731d3b3ab81c87ef81934 # v5.0.0
        with:
          images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.ORG_NAME }}/${{ env.REPO_NAME }}
          tags: ${{ env.REF_NAME }}
      # Build and push Docker image with Buildx (don't push on PR)
      # https://github.com/docker/build-push-action
      - name: Build and push Docker image
        id: build-and-push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@0565240e2d4ab88bba5387d719585280857ece09 # v5.0.0
        with:
          context: ./
          push: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max

      # Wiz Code to Cloud Integration
      # Requires gh-inner org secrets for Wiz Client ID and Secret for service account
      # Scans Container after build for vulnerabilities and
      # captures container tag to enrich Wiz and link code to cloud resources
      # For details, see: https://docs.guidehouse.com/#/essential-practices/secure-development/security-testing/wiz-seas
      - name: WIZ-Download Wiz CLI
        run: curl -o wizcli https://downloads.wiz.io/wizcli/latest/wizcli-linux-amd64 && chmod +x wizcli

      - name: WIZ-Authenticate to Wiz
        run: ./wizcli auth --id "$WIZ_CLIENT_ID" --secret "$WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET"
        env:
          WIZ_CLIENT_ID: ${{ secrets.WIZ_CLIENT_ID }}
          WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET: ${{ secrets.WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET }}

      - name: WIZ-Run wiz-cli docker image scan
        run: |
          docker pull ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          ./wizcli docker scan --image "${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}" --policy "Default vulnerabilities policy - Do not block Build step"

      - name: WIZ-Fetch digest of Docker image for Graph enrichment
        run: ./wizcli docker tag --image "${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}"
        
    outputs:
      app-docker-image: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
      guidehouse-yaml: "${{steps.extract-config.outputs.result}}"
      repo-name: "${{ env.REPO_NAME }}"
      head-ref: "${{ env.HEAD_REF }}"
      ref-name: "${{ env.REF_NAME }}"
  deploy:
    needs: build
    uses: gh-inner/actions/.github/workflows/azure-deploy-paas.yml@main
    with:
      config-name: '${{ needs.build.outputs.repo-name }}-${{ needs.build.outputs.head-ref || needs.build.outputs.ref-name }}'
      # config-tag:  ${{ github.run_id }}
      config-tag: ${{ (contains(fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).host.tag, '') && fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).host.tag) || github.run_id }}
      app-docker-image: '${{ needs.build.outputs.app-docker-image }}'
      database-docker-image: '${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.image}}'
      container-port: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).container.port}}
      database-port: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.port}}
      database-type: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.type}}
      kube-namespace: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).kube-namespace}}
      clean-install: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).clean-install}}
      teardown: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).teardown}}
      addlpodcount: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).addlpodcount}}
      aadpodidentity: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).aadpodidentity}}
      credential-name: DEV
    secrets: inherit

    secrets: inherit

===== .github\workflows\deploy-to-azure-uat.yml =====
name: Deploy to Azure - UAT

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches:
      - uat

permissions:
  id-token: write
  contents: write


env:
  # Use docker.io for Docker Hub if empty
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  # github.repository as <account>/<repo>
  IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}
  
jobs:

  build:

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v3

      # Use Cache action to cache docker builds
      - name: Cache Docker layers
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: /tmp/.buildx-cache
          key: ${{ runner.os }}-buildx-${{ github.sha }}
          restore-keys: |
            ${{ runner.os }}-buildx-
            # Set up BuildKit Docker container builder to be able to build
      # multi-platform images and export cache
      # https://github.com/docker/setup-buildx-action
      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@f95db51fddba0c2d1ec667646a06c2ce06100226 # v3.0.0

      # Login against a Docker registry except on PR
      # https://github.com/docker/login-action
      - name: Log into registry ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'
        uses: docker/login-action@343f7c4344506bcbf9b4de18042ae17996df046d # v3.0.0
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Fetch org name
        id: fetch-org-name
        run: |
          echo "ORG_NAME=${{ github.repository_owner }}" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify repo name
        id: modify-repo-name
        run: |
          repository="${{ github.repository }}"
          # Remove 'gh-inner/' prefix
          repository="${repository#gh-inner/}"
          # Replace underscores and spaces with hyphens
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | sed -e 's/[ _]/-/g')
          # Convert to lowercase
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          # Remove any trailing hyphens
          repository=$(echo "$repository" | sed -e 's/-$//')
          # Set the modified repository name as an environment variable
          echo "REPO_NAME=$repository" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify ref name
        id: modify-ref-name
        run: |
          refname=$(echo "${{ github.ref_name }}" | tr "_" "-")
          refname=$(echo "$refname" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          echo "REF_NAME=$refname" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: Modify head ref
        id: modify-head-ref
        run: |
          headref=$(echo "${{ github.head_ref }}" | tr "_" "-")
          headref=$(echo "$headref" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
          echo "HEAD_REF=$headref" >> $GITHUB_ENV

      - name: extract-config
        id: extract-config
        uses: mikefarah/yq@master
        with:
          cmd: 'yq -o=json eval .guidehouse.yaml'
        
      # Extract metadata (tags, labels) for Docker
      # https://github.com/docker/metadata-action
      - name: Extract Docker metadata
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@96383f45573cb7f253c731d3b3ab81c87ef81934 # v5.0.0
        with:
          images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.ORG_NAME }}/${{ env.REPO_NAME }}
          tags: ${{ env.REF_NAME }}
      # Build and push Docker image with Buildx (don't push on PR)
      # https://github.com/docker/build-push-action
      - name: Build and push Docker image
        id: build-and-push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@0565240e2d4ab88bba5387d719585280857ece09 # v5.0.0
        with:
          context: ./
          push: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
     
      # Wiz Code to Cloud Integration
      # Requires gh-inner org secrets for Wiz Client ID and Secret for service account
      # Scans Container after build for vulnerabilities and
      # captures container tag to enrich Wiz and link code to cloud resources
      # For details, see: https://docs.guidehouse.com/#/essential-practices/secure-development/security-testing/wiz-seas
      - name: WIZ-Download Wiz CLI
        run: curl -o wizcli https://downloads.wiz.io/wizcli/latest/wizcli-linux-amd64 && chmod +x wizcli

      - name: WIZ-Authenticate to Wiz
        run: ./wizcli auth --id "$WIZ_CLIENT_ID" --secret "$WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET"
        env:
          WIZ_CLIENT_ID: ${{ secrets.WIZ_CLIENT_ID }}
          WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET: ${{ secrets.WIZ_CLIENT_SECRET }}

      - name: WIZ-Run wiz-cli docker image scan
        run: |
          docker pull ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          ./wizcli docker scan --image "${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}" --policy "Default vulnerabilities policy - Do not block Build step"

      - name: WIZ-Fetch digest of Docker image for Graph enrichment
        run: ./wizcli docker tag --image "${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}"
        
    outputs:
      app-docker-image: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
      guidehouse-yaml: "${{steps.extract-config.outputs.result}}"
      repo-name: "${{ env.REPO_NAME }}"
      head-ref: "${{ env.HEAD_REF }}"
      ref-name: "${{ env.REF_NAME }}"
  deploy:
    needs: build
    uses: gh-inner/actions/.github/workflows/azure-deploy-paas.yml@main
    with:
      config-name: '${{ needs.build.outputs.repo-name }}-${{ needs.build.outputs.head-ref || needs.build.outputs.ref-name }}'
      # config-tag:  ${{ github.run_id }}
      config-tag: ${{ (contains(fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).host.tag, '') && fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).host.tag) || github.run_id }}
      app-docker-image: '${{ needs.build.outputs.app-docker-image }}'
      container-port: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).container.port}}
      database-docker-image: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.image}}
      database-port: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.port}}
      database-type: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).database.type}}
      kube-namespace: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).kube-namespace}}
      clean-install: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).clean-install}}
      teardown: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).teardown}}
      addlpodcount: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).addlpodcount}}
      aadpodidentity: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).aadpodidentity}}
      host-subdomain: ${{fromJson(needs.build.outputs.guidehouse-yaml).host.subdomain}}
      credential-name: UAT
    secrets: inherit

===== backend\database\__init__.py =====
"""
Database management and operations.
"""

from .manager import DatabaseManager

# Global database manager instance
db_manager = DatabaseManager()

__all__ = ["DatabaseManager", "db_manager"]

===== backend\database\manager.py =====
"""
Database connection and operations for PostgreSQL.
"""

import os
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
import asyncpg
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

# Load .env file (not configs.env for local development)
load_dotenv(".env")


class DatabaseManager:
    """Manages PostgreSQL database connections and operations."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.pool: Optional[asyncpg.Pool] = None

    async def initialize(self):
        """Initialize database connection pool."""
        host = os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost")
        port = int(os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432"))
        database = os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "pii_redaction")
        user = os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "postgres")
        password = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", "")
        
        print(f"🔌 Connecting to database: {user}@{host}:{port}/{database}")
        
        try:
            self.pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(
                host=host,
                port=port,
                database=database,
                user=user,
                password=password,
                min_size=1,
                max_size=10,  # Increased for concurrent operations
                timeout=30,  # Increased connection timeout
                command_timeout=60,  # Increased query timeout for concurrent ops
            )
            print("✅ Database pool created successfully")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Failed to create database pool: {e}")
            raise

    async def close(self):
        """Close database connection pool."""
        if self.pool:
            await self.pool.close()

    async def initialize_schema(self, force_recreate: bool = False):
        """Initialize PII redaction database schema if tables don't exist."""
        print("🗺️  Checking database schema...")
        
        # Check if PII redactions table exists
        check_table_query = """
            SELECT EXISTS (
                SELECT FROM information_schema.tables 
                WHERE table_schema = 'public' 
                AND table_name = 'pii_redactions'
            );
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            table_exists = await conn.fetchval(check_table_query)
            
            if not table_exists or force_recreate:
                if force_recreate and table_exists:
                    print("🔄 Force recreating database schema...")
                else:
                    print("📋 Database schema not found. Creating tables...")
                
                # Create schema components
                await self._create_schema(conn)
                print("✅ Database schema created successfully!")
            else:
                print("✅ Database schema already exists")

    async def _create_schema(self, conn):
        """Create the PII redaction database schema."""
        
        # Create extension for UUID generation
        await conn.execute('CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";')
        
        # Create PII redactions table
        create_pii_table_sql = '''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pii_redactions (
                id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
                
                -- File Information
                file_name VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                file_size INTEGER,
                original_blob_name VARCHAR(500),
                original_blob_url TEXT,
                redacted_blob_name VARCHAR(500),
                redacted_blob_url TEXT,
                
                -- Redaction Information
                redacted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                entities_found INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                pages_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                processing_time_seconds DECIMAL(10, 2),
                
                -- Entity Details (JSON array of detected entities)
                entities_detail JSONB,
                
                -- Review Status
                review_status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'needs_review',
                -- Options: 'needs_review', 'approved', 'rejected', 'needs_revision'
                reviewed_by VARCHAR(255),
                reviewed_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
                review_notes TEXT,
                
                -- Metadata
                created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
        '''
        
        await conn.execute(create_pii_table_sql)
        
        # Create indexes
        await self._create_indexes(conn)

        
        # Create functions and triggers
        await self._create_functions_and_triggers(conn)
        
        # Create views
        await self._create_views(conn)

    async def _create_indexes(self, conn):
        """Create database indexes for PII redaction."""
        indexes = [
            # PII Redactions indexes
            'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pii_redactions_file_name ON pii_redactions(file_name);',
            'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pii_redactions_redacted_at ON pii_redactions(redacted_at DESC);',
            'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pii_redactions_review_status ON pii_redactions(review_status);',
        ]
        
        for index_sql in indexes:
            await conn.execute(index_sql)
        print("✅ Database indexes created")

    async def _create_functions_and_triggers(self, conn):
        """Create database functions and triggers for PII redaction."""
        
        # Function to update updated_at timestamp
        update_timestamp_function = '''
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column()
            RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
            BEGIN
                NEW.updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
                RETURN NEW;
            END;
            $$ language 'plpgsql';
        '''
        await conn.execute(update_timestamp_function)
        
        # Trigger for pii_redactions updated_at
        update_pii_timestamp_trigger = '''
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS update_pii_redactions_updated_at ON pii_redactions;
            CREATE TRIGGER update_pii_redactions_updated_at 
                BEFORE UPDATE ON pii_redactions
                FOR EACH ROW
                EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
        '''
        await conn.execute(update_pii_timestamp_trigger)
        
        print("✅ Database functions and triggers created")

    async def _create_views(self, conn):
        """Create database views for PII redaction statistics."""
        
        # PII Redaction Statistics view
        pii_statistics_view = '''
            CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW pii_redaction_statistics AS
            SELECT 
                COUNT(*) as total_redactions,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status = 'needs_review') as pending_count,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status = 'approved') as approved_count,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status = 'rejected') as rejected_count,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status = 'needs_revision') as needs_revision_count,
                AVG(entities_found) as avg_entities_found,
                AVG(pages_processed) as avg_pages_processed,
                AVG(processing_time_seconds) as avg_processing_time,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE redacted_at >= CURRENT_DATE) as today_count,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE redacted_at >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '7 days') as week_count,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE redacted_at >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '30 days') as month_count,
                CASE 
                    WHEN COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status IN ('approved', 'rejected')) > 0 
                    THEN ROUND(
                        (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status = 'approved')::decimal / 
                         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE review_status IN ('approved', 'rejected'))::decimal) * 100, 
                        2
                    )
                    ELSE 0 
                END as approval_rate_percentage
            FROM pii_redactions;
        '''
        await conn.execute(pii_statistics_view)
        print("✅ Database views created")

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def get_connection(self):
        """Get a database connection from the pool."""
        async with self.pool.acquire() as connection:
            yield connection

    async def get_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get PII redaction statistics."""
        query = """
            SELECT * FROM pii_redaction_statistics
        """

        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            row = await conn.fetchrow(query)
            return dict(row) if row else {}

    # ========================================
    # PII Redaction Methods
    # ========================================

    async def insert_pii_redaction(
        self,
        file_name: str,
        file_size: int,
        original_blob_url: str,
        redacted_blob_url: str,
        entities_found: int,
        pages_processed: int,
        processing_time_seconds: float,
        entities_detail: List[Dict[str, str]]
    ) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        """
        Insert a new PII redaction record.
        Returns a tuple of (UUID, is_duplicate).
        Updates existing record if duplicate (same file_name).
        """
        import json
        
        # First check if duplicate exists
        check_query = """
            SELECT id FROM pii_redactions
            WHERE file_name = $1
            LIMIT 1
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            existing_id = await conn.fetchval(check_query, file_name)
            
            if existing_id:
                print(f"⚠️  Duplicate PII redaction found: {file_name}")
                print(f"🔄 UPDATING existing row ID: {existing_id} with new redaction data")
                
                # UPDATE the existing row with new redaction data
                update_query = """
                    UPDATE pii_redactions SET
                        file_size = $1,
                        original_blob_url = $2,
                        redacted_blob_url = $3,
                        entities_found = $4,
                        pages_processed = $5,
                        processing_time_seconds = $6,
                        entities_detail = $7::jsonb,
                        redacted_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                        review_status = 'needs_review',
                        reviewed_by = NULL,
                        reviewed_at = NULL,
                        review_notes = NULL,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                    WHERE id = $8
                    RETURNING id;
                """
                
                updated_id = await conn.fetchval(
                    update_query,
                    file_size,
                    original_blob_url,
                    redacted_blob_url,
                    entities_found,
                    pages_processed,
                    processing_time_seconds,
                    json.dumps(entities_detail),
                    existing_id
                )
                return str(updated_id), True
            
            # No duplicate, insert new record
            query = """
                INSERT INTO pii_redactions (
                    file_name, file_size, original_blob_url, redacted_blob_url,
                    entities_found, pages_processed, processing_time_seconds,
                    entities_detail, review_status
                ) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8::jsonb, 'needs_review')
                RETURNING id;
            """
            
            record_id = await conn.fetchval(
                query,
                file_name,
                file_size,
                original_blob_url,
                redacted_blob_url,
                entities_found,
                pages_processed,
                processing_time_seconds,
                json.dumps(entities_detail)  # Convert list to JSON string
            )
            return str(record_id), False

    async def get_pii_redactions(
        self,
        status_filter: Optional[str] = None,
        limit: int = 100,
        offset: int = 0
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Get list of PII redaction records with optional filtering.
        
        Args:
            status_filter: Optional review status to filter by
            limit: Maximum number of records to return
            offset: Number of records to skip
            
        Returns:
            List of PII redaction records
        """
        if status_filter:
            query = """
                SELECT 
                    id, file_name, file_size, original_blob_name, original_blob_url, 
                    redacted_blob_name, redacted_blob_url, redacted_at, entities_found, 
                    pages_processed, processing_time_seconds, entities_detail, 
                    review_status, reviewed_by, reviewed_at, review_notes,
                    created_at, updated_at
                FROM pii_redactions
                WHERE review_status = $1
                ORDER BY redacted_at DESC
                LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3;
            """
            params = [status_filter, limit, offset]
        else:
            query = """
                SELECT 
                    id, file_name, file_size, original_blob_name, original_blob_url, 
                    redacted_blob_name, redacted_blob_url, redacted_at, entities_found, 
                    pages_processed, processing_time_seconds, entities_detail, 
                    review_status, reviewed_by, reviewed_at, review_notes,
                    created_at, updated_at
                FROM pii_redactions
                ORDER BY redacted_at DESC
                LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2;
            """
            params = [limit, offset]
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            rows = await conn.fetch(query, *params)
            return [dict(row) for row in rows]

    async def get_pii_redaction_by_id(self, redaction_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Get a single PII redaction record by ID.
        
        Args:
            redaction_id: The UUID of the redaction record
            
        Returns:
            The redaction record or None if not found
        """
        query = """
            SELECT 
                id, file_name, file_size, original_blob_name, original_blob_url, 
                redacted_blob_name, redacted_blob_url, redacted_at, entities_found, 
                pages_processed, processing_time_seconds, entities_detail, 
                review_status, reviewed_by, reviewed_at, review_notes,
                created_at, updated_at
            FROM pii_redactions
            WHERE id = $1;
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            row = await conn.fetchrow(query, redaction_id)
            return dict(row) if row else None

    async def update_pii_redaction_review(
        self,
        redaction_id: str,
        review_status: str,
        reviewed_by: str,
        review_notes: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Update the review status of a PII redaction.
        
        Args:
            redaction_id: The UUID of the redaction record
            review_status: New status (needs_review, approved, rejected, needs_revision)
            reviewed_by: Username of the reviewer
            review_notes: Optional notes about the review
            
        Returns:
            True if update was successful, False if record not found
        """
        query = """
            UPDATE pii_redactions
            SET 
                review_status = $1,
                reviewed_by = $2,
                reviewed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                review_notes = $3,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = $4
            RETURNING id;
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            result = await conn.fetchval(
                query,
                review_status,     # $1: review_status
                reviewed_by,       # $2: reviewed_by
                review_notes,      # $3: review_notes (can be NULL)
                redaction_id       # $4: id (WHERE clause)
            )
            return result is not None

    async def delete_pii_redaction(self, redaction_id: str) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a PII redaction record.
        
        Args:
            redaction_id: The UUID of the redaction record
            
        Returns:
            True if deletion was successful, False if record not found
        """
        query = """
            DELETE FROM pii_redactions
            WHERE id = $1
            RETURNING id;
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            result = await conn.fetchval(query, redaction_id)
            return result is not None

    async def update_pii_redaction_blob_info(
        self,
        redaction_id: str,
        redacted_blob_name: str,
        redacted_blob_url: str
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Update blob storage information for a PII redaction record.
        
        Args:
            redaction_id: The UUID of the redaction record
            redacted_blob_name: The name of the blob in storage
            redacted_blob_url: The URL to access the blob
            
        Returns:
            True if update was successful, False if record not found
        """
        query = """
            UPDATE pii_redactions
            SET 
                redacted_blob_name = $1,
                redacted_blob_url = $2,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = $3
            RETURNING id;
        """
        
        async with self.get_connection() as conn:
            result = await conn.fetchval(
                query,
                redacted_blob_name,   # $1: redacted_blob_name
                redacted_blob_url,    # $2: redacted_blob_url
                redaction_id          # $3: id (WHERE clause)
            )
            return result is not None


# Global database manager instance
db_manager = DatabaseManager()

===== backend\prompts\legal_document_redaction.txt =====
You are a legal document redaction assistant specializing in Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 5.2 and HIPAA regulations compliance. Your task is to analyze the following text and identify all instances of sensitive personal information in court documents. Aim for 95% accuracy and err on the side of over-redaction (better to redact too much than miss sensitive information).

Specifically, you must identify the following information that requires redaction:

**Disclaimer**: All example values, scenarios, names, locations, and other illustrative details referenced in the following documentation and docstrings are entirely fictional and provided solely for demonstration purposes. Any resemblance to real persons, organizations, events, or locations is purely coincidental and unintended. No portion of this material should be interpreted as referencing actual entities or individuals.

**CATEGORY 1: COMPLETE DATES (extract full date as written)**
**IMPORTANT**: For ALL date entries, you must extract the COMPLETE date exactly as it appears in the document. Do NOT extract only the year - extract the full date including month, day, and year in whatever format it appears. The system will automatically format these for redaction afterwards.

1. Birth dates - identify the COMPLETE birth date in any format (e.g., "02/24/1985", "March 14, 1985", "DOB 07/22/1990", "date of birth is January 3, 2000", "12/31/1995", "born on 5/15/1988"). Extract the ENTIRE date string, NOT just the year. Examples of what to extract:
   - From "DOB: 02/24/1985" → extract "02/24/1985"
   - From "born on March 14, 1985" → extract "March 14, 1985"
   - From "date of birth is 12/31/2000" → extract "12/31/2000"
   - From "January 3, 1990" → extract "January 3, 1990"
   - DO NOT extract only "1985" - you must include month and day
   - DO NOT extract only the year portion

**CATEGORY 2: IDENTIFIERS (extract complete numbers)**
2. Social Security numbers - identify full SSN (e.g., "123-45-6789", "SSN 123456789", "123-45-6789"). EXCLUDE already-redacted formats like "XXX-XX-6789"
3. Taxpayer identification numbers - identify full TIN with 9 digits in 2-7 format (e.g., "12-3456789", "TIN 12-3456789", "12-3456789"). EXCLUDE already-redacted formats like "XX-XXXXXXX" or "XX-XXX6789"
4. Financial account numbers - identify BOTH full account numbers (e.g., "account number 123456789", "account 987654321") AND partial references (e.g., "credit card ending 6789", "card ending in 6789"). These will be formatted to show only last 4 digits or mask the digits with ****
5. Health plan beneficiary numbers - identify insurance member IDs and health plan numbers (e.g., "member ID 12345678", "health plan #ABC123456")
6. Insurance policy numbers - identify all insurance policy identifiers (e.g., "policy number POL123456", "auto policy 987654321")
7. Certificate/license numbers - identify professional licenses, certifications (e.g., "license #12345", "certificate ABC789")
8. Passport numbers - identify full passport numbers (e.g., "YCZ4FC3N2", "passport X12345678", "AB1234567"). EXCLUDE already-redacted formats like "Pass: ****5678"
9. Driver's license numbers - identify full DL numbers (e.g., "TX10469877", "TX12345678", "CA98765432"). EXCLUDE already-redacted formats like "DL: ****4321"
10. Vehicle identifiers and serial numbers - identify license plate numbers, VINs (e.g., "license plate ABC123", "VIN 1HGBH41JXMN109186")
11. Device identifiers and serial numbers - identify equipment serial numbers, device IDs (e.g., "device serial SN123456", "equipment ID EQ789")

**CATEGORY 3: NAMES (ONLY WITH EXPLICIT CONTEXT)**
**CRITICAL**: Only identify names when they appear with EXPLICIT contextual indicators. Do NOT identify random names without context.
12. Names of minors - identify ONLY when explicitly described as minor, child, juvenile, or under 18 (e.g., "the minor John Doe", "child named Jane Smith", "juvenile A.B.", "minor J.D."). Include both full names and initials. DO NOT identify names without minor context.
13. Party names - identify ONLY when explicitly described as plaintiff, defendant, appellant, appellee, petitioner, or respondent in body text (e.g., "plaintiff John Smith", "defendant Jane Doe", "the appellant Mary Jones"). DO NOT identify names in case captions or without party role context.
14. Witness names - identify ONLY when explicitly described as witness, testified, deponent, or provided testimony (e.g., "witness John Smith testified", "deponent Jane Doe stated", "testimony of Mary Jones"). DO NOT identify names without witness context.
15. Victim names - identify ONLY when explicitly described as victim, injured party, or complainant (e.g., "victim John Smith", "injured party Jane Doe", "complainant Mary Jones"). DO NOT identify names without victim context.

**CATEGORY 4: ADDRESSES & CONTACT INFORMATION**
16. Home addresses - identify the COMPLETE street address including the street number, street name, city, state, and zip code (e.g., "1234 Maple Street, Austin, TX 987654", "56 Oak Lane, Dallas", "property located at 789 Pine Ave., New York, NY 10001"). You MUST include the full address starting from the street number through the zip code if present. DO NOT identify only city/state - you must capture the entire address. DO NOT identify company names that start with ordinals like "5th Avenue Partners"
17. Telephone numbers - identify personal phone numbers (exclude business numbers published in directories)
18. Fax numbers - identify personal fax numbers
19. Email addresses - identify personal email addresses (exclude business emails in official capacity)
20. Web URLs - identify personal websites and social media profiles
21. IP addresses - identify internet protocol addresses associated with individuals

**CATEGORY 5: HEALTH & BIOMETRIC INFORMATION**
22. Medical diagnoses - identify ONLY the specific disease/condition name, NOT surrounding context words (e.g., from "diagnosed with Type II diabetes" extract "Type II diabetes", from "treatment for fractured femur" extract "fractured femur", from "diagnosed with migraine disorder" extract "migraine disorder")
23. Medical treatments - identify ONLY the specific procedure/medication name (e.g., from "underwent knee replacement surgery" extract "knee replacement surgery", from "prescription for Lisinopril 10mg" extract "Lisinopril 10mg")
24. Mental health information - identify ONLY the specific condition/treatment (e.g., from "therapy sessions for anxiety and depression" extract "anxiety and depression")
25. Medical test results - identify ONLY the specific result (e.g., from "cholesterol levels at 245 mg/dL" extract "245 mg/dL")
26. Medical record numbers - identify patient IDs and medical record identifiers
27. Biometric identifiers - identify textual references to biometric data such as fingerprint IDs, DNA sample references, retinal scan IDs, voiceprint analysis IDs, and facial recognition data identifiers

**CATEGORY 6: EMPLOYMENT & FINANCIAL**
28. Employment addresses - identify workplace street addresses with 3+ digit street numbers but NOT employer names (e.g., in "worked at ABC Corp., 123 Main St., Houston" - identify "123 Main St., Houston" only). DO NOT identify company names starting with ordinals like "5th Avenue Partners" as addresses
29. Income and salary - identify ONLY the dollar amount with precision (e.g., from "annual income was $85,000" extract "$85,000", from "salary of $150,000" extract "$150,000", from "$106,000 annual income" extract "$106,000")
30. Bank balances - identify ONLY the dollar amount with cents (e.g., from "bank balance of $12,345.67" extract "$12,345.67", from "$35,994.90 in account" extract "$35,994.90")
31. Investment details - identify ONLY the specific number and unit (e.g., from "200 shares of ABC stock valued at $10,000" extract "200 shares" and "$10,000" separately, from "holdings of 294 shares" extract "294 shares")

**CATEGORY 7: TRADE SECRETS & PROPRIETARY INFO**
32. Proprietary algorithms - identify the algorithm/function name INCLUDING any surrounding quotes or special characters (e.g., from "algorithm 'WeightedRiskScore()' utilizes" extract "'WeightedRiskScore()'", from "'OptiCalc' formula" extract "'OptiCalc'")
33. Manufacturing processes - identify specific methods (e.g., "three-step chemical treatment involving Compound A, Compound B")
34. Customer lists - identify client information (e.g., "client list includes Fortune 500 companies")
35. Pricing models - identify discount structures (e.g., "negotiated discounts ranging from 15% to 30%")
36. Design specifications - identify textual references to technical drawings and specifications (e.g., "CAD drawing #DWG-123456", "specification TS-789")
37. Proprietary formulas - identify secret recipes/formulas (e.g., "beverage formula contains unique ratio of sweeteners")

**CATEGORY 8: PERSONAL RELATIONSHIPS & CHARACTERISTICS**
38. Personal relationships - identify details about family relationships, marriages, divorces, domestic partnerships
39. Unique identifying characteristics - identify any other unique number, characteristic, or code that could identify an individual

**IMPORTANT - DO NOT IDENTIFY FOR REDACTION:**
- Judge names (e.g., "Judge Williams presided")
- Attorney names and bar numbers (e.g., "Sarah Martinez, Bar #234567")
- Court names and locations (e.g., "Superior Court of California")
- Case numbers and docket numbers (e.g., "Case No. 23-CV-12345")
- Legal citations (e.g., "Code of Civil Procedure § 2031.310", "347 U.S. 483")
- Filing dates and hearing dates (e.g., "filed on August 15, 2023")
- Signature blocks of attorneys or court officials
- Employer names WITHOUT addresses (e.g., "ABC Corp" is OK, "ABC Corp, 123 Main St" - redact address only)
- Business entity names (e.g., "Acme Corporation")
- Public official names when relevant to case
- Expert witness names mentioned in professional capacity without vulnerable context
- Names appearing WITHOUT explicit context indicators (e.g., random names in examples, citations, or general references)
- Names of authors, researchers, or professionals cited in documents
- Names in email addresses or usernames (e.g., "john.doe@example.com" - redact as email, not as name)
- City and state WITHOUT street address (e.g., "Austin, TX" is OK, "1234 Maple St, Austin, TX" - redact street only)
- Names already in initial format (e.g., "A.B.", "C.D.", "E.F." - treat these as ALREADY REDACTED and do not identify them, UNLESS the text explicitly identifies the person as a minor, party, witness, or victim; in that case, follow Category 3 and include the initials as the name to redact)
- Account numbers already showing only last 4 digits (e.g., "account ending in 4053", "Acct: ****4053" - these are ALREADY REDACTED)
- SSNs already showing only last 4 digits (e.g., "XXX-XX-6789", "SSN ending in 6789" - these are ALREADY REDACTED)
- Any identifier formatted with asterisks/X's and last 4 digits (e.g., "****4053", "XXX-XX-6789" - these are ALREADY REDACTED)
- Business phone numbers and fax numbers published in directories
- Business email addresses in official capacity
- Business websites and corporate URLs

**REDACTION INSTRUCTIONS:**
For each item found, determine its category to ensure proper formatting:
- SSN/TIN/DL/Passport/Account → last 4 digits preserved
- DOB → year only preserved
- Minor names → initials only
- Addresses → city & state only
- Medical → generic description
- Employment → employer name only (remove address)
- Financial details → generic summary
- Trade secrets → mark as confidential

Return results in JSON format with optional context for verification. Be aggressive in identification - if something MIGHT be sensitive, include it:

[
  {{"type": "<specific type from categories above>", "value": "<exact text to redact>", "context": "<brief surrounding text for verification (optional)>"}},
  ...
]

Here is the text to analyze:
{text}
