===== backend\services\blob_storage.py =====
"""
Azure Blob Storage Service
Handles document uploads and downloads to Azure Blob Storage
"""

import os
import logging
from typing import Optional, BinaryIO
from datetime import datetime
from azure.storage.blob import BlobServiceClient, BlobClient, ContainerClient
from azure.core.exceptions import ResourceExistsError, ResourceNotFoundError

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class BlobStorageService:
    """Service for managing document uploads to Azure Blob Storage"""

    def __init__(self):
        """Initialize blob storage service with connection string from environment"""
        self.connection_string = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONNECTION_STRING")
        
        if not self.connection_string:
            raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONNECTION_STRING not found in environment variables")
        
        try:
            self.blob_service_client = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(
                self.connection_string
            )
            logger.info(f"✅ Blob storage initialized")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to initialize blob storage: {e}")
            raise



    def upload_pii_redacted_document(self, file_content: bytes, filename: str, metadata: Optional[dict] = None) -> dict:
        """
        Upload a PII redacted document to the dedicated PII redacted container
        
        Args:
            file_content: Binary content of the file
            filename: Name of the file
            metadata: Optional metadata to attach to the blob
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary with upload information including blob_name and blob_url
        """
        try:
            pii_container = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED")
            if not pii_container:
                raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED not found in environment variables")
            
            # Ensure container exists
            self._ensure_container_exists(pii_container)
            
            # Generate blob name with timestamp
            timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            blob_name = f"{timestamp}_{filename}"
            
            # Upload to blob storage
            blob_client = self.blob_service_client.get_blob_client(
                container=pii_container,
                blob=blob_name
            )
            
            blob_client.upload_blob(
                file_content,
                overwrite=True,
                metadata=metadata or {}
            )
            
            blob_url = blob_client.url
            logger.info(f"✅ Uploaded PII redacted document to blob: {blob_name}")
            
            return {
                "blob_name": blob_name,
                "blob_url": blob_url,
                "container": pii_container,
                "filename": filename,
                "uploaded_at": timestamp
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to upload PII redacted document to blob storage: {e}")
            raise

    def download_pii_redacted_document(self, blob_name: str) -> bytes:
        """
        Download a PII redacted document from the dedicated PII redacted container
        
        Args:
            blob_name: The name of the blob to download
            
        Returns:
            Binary content of the file
        """
        try:
            pii_container = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED")
            if not pii_container:
                raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED not found in environment variables")
            
            blob_client = self.blob_service_client.get_blob_client(
                container=pii_container,
                blob=blob_name
            )
            
            download_stream = blob_client.download_blob()
            content = download_stream.readall()
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Downloaded PII redacted document from blob: {blob_name}")
            return content
            
        except ResourceNotFoundError:
            logger.error(f"❌ PII redacted blob not found: {blob_name}")
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to download PII redacted document from blob storage: {e}")
            raise

    def get_pii_redacted_blob_url(self, blob_name: str) -> str:
        """
        Get the URL for a PII redacted blob
        
        Args:
            blob_name: The name of the blob
            
        Returns:
            The blob URL
        """
        pii_container = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED")
        if not pii_container:
            raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED not found in environment variables")
        blob_client = self.blob_service_client.get_blob_client(
            container=pii_container,
            blob=blob_name
        )
        return blob_client.url

    def list_pii_redacted_documents(self) -> list:
        """
        List all PII redacted documents in the container
        
        Returns:
            List of dictionaries with blob information
        """
        try:
            pii_container = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED")
            if not pii_container:
                raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED not found in environment variables")
            
            container_client = self.blob_service_client.get_container_client(pii_container)
            
            blobs = []
            for blob in container_client.list_blobs():
                blobs.append({
                    "name": blob.name,
                    "size": blob.size,
                    "last_modified": blob.last_modified.isoformat() if blob.last_modified else None,
                    "content_type": getattr(blob, 'content_settings', {}).get('content_type') if hasattr(blob, 'content_settings') else None,
                    "metadata": blob.metadata or {}
                })
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Listed {len(blobs)} PII redacted documents")
            return blobs
            
        except ResourceNotFoundError:
            logger.warning(f"❌ PII redacted container not found: {pii_container}")
            return []
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to list PII redacted documents: {e}")
            raise

    def delete_pii_redacted_document(self, blob_name: str) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a PII redacted document from the container
        
        Args:
            blob_name: The name of the blob to delete
            
        Returns:
            True if deleted successfully, False if not found
        """
        try:
            pii_container = os.getenv("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED")
            if not pii_container:
                raise ValueError("AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_PII_REDACTED not found in environment variables")
            
            blob_client = self.blob_service_client.get_blob_client(
                container=pii_container,
                blob=blob_name
            )
            
            blob_client.delete_blob(delete_snapshots="include")
            logger.info(f"✅ Deleted PII redacted document: {blob_name}")
            return True
            
        except ResourceNotFoundError:
            logger.warning(f"❌ PII redacted blob not found for deletion: {blob_name}")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to delete PII redacted document: {e}")
            raise

    def _ensure_container_exists(self, container_name: str, metadata: Optional[dict] = None) -> bool:
        """
        Ensure a container exists, creating it if necessary (private method)
        
        Args:
            container_name: Name of the container
            metadata: Optional metadata for the container
            
        Returns:
            True if container was created, False if it already existed
        """
        try:
            container_client = self.blob_service_client.get_container_client(container_name)
            container_client.create_container(metadata=metadata or {})
            logger.info(f"✅ Created new container: {container_name}")
            return True
            
        except ResourceExistsError:
            logger.info(f"ℹ️  Container already exists: {container_name}")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to ensure container exists {container_name}: {e}")
            raise

    def get_container_info(self, container_name: str) -> dict:
        """
        Get detailed information about a container
        
        Args:
            container_name: Name of the container
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary with container information
        """
        try:
            container_client = self.blob_service_client.get_container_client(container_name)
            properties = container_client.get_container_properties()
            
            # Count blobs in container
            blob_count = sum(1 for _ in container_client.list_blobs())
            
            return {
                "name": container_name,
                "last_modified": properties.last_modified.isoformat() if properties.last_modified else None,
                "metadata": properties.metadata or {},
                "blob_count": blob_count,
                "lease_status": properties.lease.status if hasattr(properties, 'lease') else None
            }
            
        except ResourceNotFoundError:
            logger.warning(f"❌ Container not found: {container_name}")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to get container info for {container_name}: {e}")
            raise

# Global instance
_blob_storage_service: Optional[BlobStorageService] = None

def get_blob_storage_service() -> BlobStorageService:
    """Get or create the global blob storage service instance"""
    global _blob_storage_service
    if _blob_storage_service is None:
        _blob_storage_service = BlobStorageService()
    return _blob_storage_service

===== backend\services\pii_redaction.py =====
"""
PII/PHI Redaction Service for Legal Documents.
Follows FRCP 5.2 redaction standards for court documents.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import base64
import random
import string
import fitz  # PyMuPDF
from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any
from openai import AzureOpenAI
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv(".env")


# Configuration constants
DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS = 1500
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE = 0.1


def get_azure_client() -> AzureOpenAI:
    """Create and return Azure OpenAI client."""
    return AzureOpenAI(
        api_version=os.getenv("OPENAI_API_VERSION", "2025-01-01-preview"),
        azure_endpoint=os.getenv("AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT"),
        api_key=os.getenv("AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY")
    )


def load_prompt_template() -> str:
    """Load the PII detection prompt template."""
    prompt_path = os.path.join(
        os.path.dirname(__file__),
        "..", "prompts", "legal_document_redaction.txt"
    )
    
    if not os.path.exists(prompt_path):
        # Fallback inline prompt
        return """Extract all Personally Identifiable Information (PII) and Protected Health Information (PHI) from this legal document text.

            Text to analyze:
            {text}

            Return a JSON array of detected entities with this exact format:
            [
            {{"type": "Social Security Number", "value": "123-45-6789"}},
            {{"type": "Date of Birth", "value": "01/15/1985"}},
            {{"type": "Minor Name", "value": "John Smith"}},
            {{"type": "Home Address", "value": "123 Main St, Austin, TX 78701"}},
            {{"type": "Financial Account Number", "value": "1234567890"}},
            {{"type": "Medical Information", "value": "diabetes diagnosis"}},
            {{"type": "Employment Information", "value": "Works at ABC Corp, 456 Business Rd"}},
            {{"type": "Financial Details", "value": "salary of $106,000"}},
            {{"type": "Trade Secret", "value": "XYZ Algorithm"}},
            {{"type": "Passport Number", "value": "P12345678"}},
            {{"type": "Driver License Number", "value": "DL1234567"}}
            ]

            DO NOT include:
            - Judge names
            - Attorney names  
            - Case numbers
            - Legal citations
            - Court names

            Return ONLY valid JSON array, no other text."""
    
    with open(prompt_path, 'r') as f:
        return f.read()


class LegalAnonymizer:
    """
    Anonymizer for court documents following FRCP 5.2 redaction requirements.
    
    Formatting Rules:
    - SSN/TIN: Show last 4 digits (XXX-XX-6789)
    - DOB: Show year only (1985)
    - Minors: Use initials (A.B.)
    - Accounts: Show last 4 digits (****4321)
    - Addresses: City & state only (Austin, TX)
    - Passport: Show last 4 digits (P****5678)
    - Driver's License: Show last 4 digits (****4321)
    - Medical: Generic description ([CONFIDENTIAL - MEDICAL INFORMATION])
    - Employment: Employer name only, remove address
    - Financial: Generic summary ([REDACTED - FINANCIAL DETAILS])
    - Trade Secrets: Mark as confidential ([CONFIDENTIAL - TRADE SECRET])
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.minor_counter = 0
        self.name_counter = 0
        self.witness_counter = 0
        self.employer_map = {}
        self.mapping = {}
    
    def anonymize_by_type(self, value: str, entity_type: str) -> str:
        """Anonymize based on entity type with court-specific formatting."""
        entity_lower = entity_type.lower()
        
        # SSN & Taxpayer ID: XXX-XX-6789 (last 4 digits)
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["social security", "ssn", "taxpayer", "tin"]):
            return self._redact_ssn(value)
        
        # Birth dates: DOB: 1985 (year only)
        if "birth" in entity_lower or "dob" in entity_lower or "date" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_dob(value)
        
        # Minor names: A.B. (initials)
        if "minor" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_minor_name(value)
        
        # Financial accounts: Acct: ****4321 (last 4 digits)
        if "account" in entity_lower or "financial" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_account(value)
        
        # Passport: Pass: ****5678 (last 4 digits)
        if "passport" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_passport(value)
        
        # Driver's License: DL: ****4321 (last 4 digits)
        if "driver" in entity_lower or "dl" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_drivers_license(value)
        
        # Certificate/License Numbers: ****4321 (last 4 digits)
        if "certificate" in entity_lower or "license" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_certificate(value)
        
        # Vehicle & Device Identifiers: ****4321 (last 4 chars)
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["vehicle", "device", "serial", "vin", "equipment"]):
            return self._redact_vehicle_device(value)
        
        # Health/Insurance IDs: ****4321 (last 4 digits)
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["medical record", "health plan", "beneficiary", "insurance policy", "policy number", "member id"]):
            return self._redact_health_insurance_id(value)
        
        # Contact Information: Full redaction for privacy
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["phone", "telephone", "fax", "email", "url", "ip address"]):
            return self._redact_contact_info(entity_lower, value)
        
        # Biometric Identifier References: Generic marker
        if "biometric" in entity_lower or "fingerprint" in entity_lower or "dna" in entity_lower or "retinal" in entity_lower or "voiceprint" in entity_lower:
            return "[BIOMETRIC ID]"
        
        # Personal Relationships: Generic marker
        if "relationship" in entity_lower or "marriage" in entity_lower or "divorce" in entity_lower or "domestic partner" in entity_lower:
            return "[RELATIONSHIP]"
        
        # Home addresses: Austin, TX (city & state only)
        if "address" in entity_lower and "employment" not in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_home_address(value)
        
        # Employment addresses: Keep employer name, remove address
        if "employment" in entity_lower or "employer" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_employment_address(value)
        
        # Medical information: Generic description
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["medical", "health", "diagnosis", "treatment", "mental", "test"]):
            return self._redact_medical(entity_lower, value)
        
        # Income/Salary: Keep the specific value (e.g., "$106,000")
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["income", "salary", "balance", "investment"]):
            return self._redact_financial_details(entity_lower, value)
        
        # Trade secrets: Keep the specific algorithm/formula name
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["proprietary", "algorithm", "formula", "trade secret", "customer list", "pricing", "design", "manufacturing"]):
            return self._redact_trade_secret(entity_lower, value)
        
        # Party/Witness names: Initials or identifiers
        if any(x in entity_lower for x in ["plaintiff", "defendant", "party"]):
            return self._redact_party_name(value)
        
        if "witness" in entity_lower or "victim" in entity_lower:
            return self._redact_witness_name(value)
        
        # Default: Generic redaction
        return "[REDACTED]"
    
    def _redact_ssn(self, ssn: str) -> str:
        """Format SSN/TIN showing last 4 digits: XXX-XX-1234 or XX-XXX1234."""
        digits = ''.join(c for c in ssn if c.isdigit())
        
        # Check if it's a TIN (has "TIN" keyword or 2-digit prefix)
        has_tin_keyword = 'tin' in ssn.lower()
        has_tin_format = (len(digits) == 9 and '-' in ssn
                          and ssn.index('-') <= 2)
        is_tin = has_tin_keyword or has_tin_format
        
        # TIN format: XX-XXX1234 (show last 4 digits)
        if is_tin and len(digits) == 9:
            return f"XX-XXX{digits[-4:]}"
        # SSN format: XXX-XX-XXXX (9 digits, 3-2-4 format)
        elif len(digits) >= 4:
            return f"XXX-XX-{digits[-4:]}"
        return "XXX-XX-XXXX"
    
    def _redact_dob(self, dob: str) -> str:
        """Format date of birth showing year only."""
        # Look for 4-digit year
        year_match = re.search(r'\b(19|20)\d{2}\b', dob)
        if year_match:
            year = year_match.group(0)
            return year
        return "[YEAR]"
    
    def _redact_minor_name(self, name: str) -> str:
        """Format minor name as initials: J.S."""
        if name in self.mapping:
            return self.mapping[name]
        
        # Check if already in initial format (e.g., "A.B.", "C.D.")
        # Pattern: single letter followed by period, repeated
        if re.match(r'^[A-Z]\.[A-Z]\.$', name.strip()):
            # Already in initials format, return as-is
            return name.strip()
        
        # Generate initials from name
        words = name.strip().split()
        if len(words) >= 2:
            initials = '.'.join(w[0].upper() for w in words if w) + '.'
        elif len(words) == 1 and len(words[0]) > 0:
            initials = words[0][0].upper() + '.D.'
        else:
            self.minor_counter += 1
            initials = f"Minor #{self.minor_counter}"
        
        self.mapping[name] = initials
        return initials
    
    def _redact_account(self, account: str) -> str:
        """Format account number showing last 4 digits."""
        # Handle "ending XXXX" or "ending in XXXX" patterns
        if "ending" in account.lower():
            # Replace digits with asterisks: "ending 4053" -> "ending ****"
            return re.sub(r'\d+', '****', account)
        
        # Handle full account numbers
        digits = ''.join(c for c in account if c.isdigit())
        if len(digits) >= 4:
            return f"****{digits[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_passport(self, passport: str) -> str:
        """Format passport showing last 4 digits."""
        # Extract alphanumeric characters
        alnum = ''.join(c for c in passport if c.isalnum())
        if len(alnum) >= 4:
            return f"****{alnum[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_drivers_license(self, dl: str) -> str:
        """Format driver's license showing last 4 digits."""
        alnum = ''.join(c for c in dl if c.isalnum())
        if len(alnum) >= 4:
            return f"****{alnum[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_certificate(self, cert: str) -> str:
        """Format certificate/license number showing last 4 digits."""
        alnum = ''.join(c for c in cert if c.isalnum())
        if len(alnum) >= 4:
            return f"****{alnum[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_vehicle_device(self, identifier: str) -> str:
        """Format vehicle/device identifier showing last 4 characters."""
        alnum = ''.join(c for c in identifier if c.isalnum())
        if len(alnum) >= 4:
            return f"****{alnum[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_health_insurance_id(self, id_value: str) -> str:
        """Format health/insurance ID showing last 4 digits."""
        alnum = ''.join(c for c in id_value if c.isalnum())
        if len(alnum) >= 4:
            return f"****{alnum[-4:]}"
        return "****XXXX"
    
    def _redact_contact_info(self, entity_type: str, value: str) -> str:
        """Redact contact information with appropriate markers."""
        if "phone" in entity_type or "telephone" in entity_type:
            return "[PHONE]"
        elif "fax" in entity_type:
            return "[FAX]"
        elif "email" in entity_type:
            return "[EMAIL]"
        elif "url" in entity_type or "website" in entity_type:
            return "[URL]"
        elif "ip" in entity_type:
            return "[IP ADDRESS]"
        else:
            return "[CONTACT INFO]"
    
    def _redact_home_address(self, address: str) -> str:
        """Show city & state only from address."""
        # Try to extract city, state pattern
        match = re.search(r'([A-Z][a-zA-Z\s]+),\s*([A-Z]{2})', address)
        if match:
            city = match.group(1).strip()
            state = match.group(2)
            return f"{city}, {state}"
        return "[ADDRESS]"
    
    def _redact_employment_address(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Remove employment address completely, keep only company name."""
        text = text.strip()
        
        # Check if it's purely a street address (no company name)
        # Pattern: starts with 3+ digit street number like "123 Main St"
        # Exclude ordinal numbers like "5th" which are 1-2 digits + "th/st/nd/rd"
        if re.match(r'^\d{3,}\s+[A-Z]', text):
            # It's just an address, remove it completely with brackets
            return "[ADDRESS]"
        
        # Extract employer name before comma and address number
        # Handles: "Company Name, 123 Street" or "5th Ave Corp, 789..."
        if ',' in text:
            parts = text.split(',', 1)
            employer = parts[0].strip()
            # Check if the part after comma starts with street number
            if len(parts) > 1 and re.match(r'^\s*\d+\s', parts[1]):
                return employer
            # Otherwise just return the first part
            return employer
        
        # No comma, return as-is (just company name)
        return text
    
    def _redact_party_name(self, name: str) -> str:
        """Format party name as initials: J.S."""
        if name in self.mapping:
            return self.mapping[name]
        
        # Generate initials from name
        words = name.strip().split()
        if len(words) >= 2:
            initials = '.'.join(w[0].upper() for w in words if w) + '.'
        elif len(words) == 1 and len(words[0]) > 0:
            initials = words[0][0].upper() + '.S.'
        else:
            self.name_counter += 1
            initials = f"Party-{self.name_counter}"
        
        self.mapping[name] = initials
        return initials
    
    def _redact_witness_name(self, name: str) -> str:
        """Format witness name as Witness-1, Witness-2, etc."""
        if name in self.mapping:
            return self.mapping[name]
        
        self.witness_counter += 1
        witness_id = f"Witness-{self.witness_counter}"
        self.mapping[name] = witness_id
        return witness_id
    
    def _redact_medical(self, entity_type: str, value: str) -> str:
        """Generic description for medical information."""
        if "diagnos" in entity_type or "condition" in entity_type:
            return "[DIAGNOSIS]"
        elif "treatment" in entity_type or "procedure" in entity_type:
            return "[TREATMENT]"
        elif "mental" in entity_type:
            return "[MENTAL HEALTH]"
        elif "test" in entity_type or "lab" in entity_type:
            return "[LAB RESULT]"
        else:
            return "[MEDICAL INFO]"
    
    def _redact_financial_details(self, entity_type: str, value: str) -> str:
        """Return descriptive financial redaction markers."""
        entity_lower = entity_type.lower()
        # Use descriptive markers for different financial types
        if "income" in entity_lower or "salary" in entity_lower:
            return "[INCOME]"
        elif "balance" in entity_lower:
            return "[BALANCE]"
        elif "investment" in entity_lower or "shares" in entity_lower:
            # For shares, we'll only redact the number, so return just [#]
            return "[#]"
        else:
            return "[FINANCIAL]"
    
    def _redact_trade_secret(self, entity_type: str, value: str) -> str:
        """Return [PROPRIETARY] marker."""
        return "[PROPRIETARY]"


def extract_text_from_pdf(doc: fitz.Document) -> str:
    """Extract all text from PDF document."""
    text_parts = []
    for page_num, page in enumerate(doc):
        page_text = page.get_text()
        text_parts.append(page_text)
        print(f"📄 Page {page_num + 1}: {len(page_text)} characters extracted")
    
    full_text = "\n".join(text_parts)
    print(f"📚 Total text extracted: {len(full_text)} characters from {len(text_parts)} pages")
    
    if len(full_text.strip()) == 0:
        print("⚠️ WARNING: No text extracted from PDF - may be image-based or encrypted")
    
    return full_text


def deduplicate_entities(entities: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    """Remove duplicate entities with the same (case-insensitive) value to prevent redundant redactions."""
    if not entities:
        return []

    # Keep order stable while removing exact duplicates of the value (case-insensitive).
    unique_entities: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    seen_values = set()

    for entity in entities:
        raw_value = entity.get('value', '')
        value = raw_value.strip()
        if not value:
            continue

        normalized_value = value.lower()

        if normalized_value in seen_values:
            # Exact duplicate of a previously seen value; skip it.
            print(f"🔍 Skipping duplicate entity value: '{value}'")
            continue

        unique_entities.append(entity)
        seen_values.add(normalized_value)
    print(f"✅ Deduplicated: {len(entities)} → {len(unique_entities)} entities")
    return unique_entities


def rectangles_overlap(rect1: fitz.Rect, rect2: fitz.Rect, threshold: float = 2.0) -> bool:
    """Check if two rectangles overlap with a small threshold for margins."""
    return not (rect1.x1 < rect2.x0 - threshold or 
                rect1.x0 > rect2.x1 + threshold or 
                rect1.y1 < rect2.y0 - threshold or 
                rect1.y0 > rect2.y1 + threshold)


def extract_pii_entities(client: AzureOpenAI, text: str, prompt_template: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    """Call Azure OpenAI to extract PII/PHI entities from text."""
    try:
        prompt = prompt_template.format(text=text)
        
        system_msg = (
            "You are a legal document redaction expert. Extract PII/PHI entities from the document text. "
            "Return ONLY a valid JSON array with this exact format: [{\"type\": \"...\", \"value\": \"...\"}]. "
            "If no entities found, return empty array: []"
        )
        
        print(f"🤖 Calling Azure OpenAI with {len(text)} characters of text")
        print(f"🔧 Using deployment: {os.getenv('AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT_NAME', 'gpt-4.1')}")
        
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=os.getenv("AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT_NAME", "gpt-4.1"),
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": system_msg},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
            ],
            max_tokens=DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS,
            temperature=DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE,
        )
        
        content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
        print(f"📝 LLM Response length: {len(content)} characters")
        print(f"📝 LLM Response preview: {content[:200]}...")
        
        # Parse JSON response
        try:
            # Try to extract JSON array from response
            json_match = re.search(r'\[.*\]', content, re.DOTALL)
            if json_match:
                entities = json.loads(json_match.group())
                print(f"✅ Successfully parsed {len(entities)} entities from JSON match")
                return entities
            
            # Try parsing the entire content as JSON
            entities = json.loads(content)
            print(f"✅ Successfully parsed {len(entities)} entities from full content")
            return entities
            
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            print(f"❌ JSON parsing failed: {e}")
            print(f"❌ Raw content that failed to parse: {content[:500]}")
            raise ValueError("LLM returned non-JSON response") from e
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Error in extract_pii_entities: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise


def apply_redactions_to_pdf(
    doc: fitz.Document,
    output_path: str,
    entities: List[Dict[str, str]],
    anonymizer: LegalAnonymizer,
    case_sensitive: bool = True
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Apply redactions to PDF and save output."""
    redaction_count = 0
    redaction_details = []
    pages_with_redactions = set()
    text_insertions = []  # Store text to insert after redactions
    
    # Deduplicate entities to prevent over-redaction
    entities = deduplicate_entities(entities)
    
    # Track redacted regions per page to prevent overlapping redactions
    redacted_regions = {}  # page_num -> [list of rects]
    
    for entity in entities:
        entity_type = entity.get("type", "")
        original_value = entity.get("value", "")
        
        if not original_value:
            continue
        
        # Get replacement text
        replacement = anonymizer.anonymize_by_type(original_value, entity_type)
        
        # For investment/shares, only search for and redact the numeric portion
        entity_lower = entity_type.lower()
        search_value = original_value
        if "investment" in entity_lower or "shares" in entity_lower or "stock" in entity_lower:
            # Extract just the numbers to search for (e.g., "294" from "294 shares")
            numbers = re.findall(r'[\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?', original_value)
            if numbers:
                search_value = numbers[0]  # Search for just the number
                # replacement is already "[#]" from _redact_financial_details
        
        # For credit cards with "ending" pattern, only redact the digits
        elif ("credit card" in entity_lower or "account" in entity_lower) and "ending" in original_value.lower():
            # Extract just the numbers (e.g., "7890" from "credit card ending 7890")
            numbers = re.findall(r'\d+', original_value)
            if numbers:
                search_value = numbers[-1]  # Search for the last number (the actual digits)
                replacement = "****"  # Replace just the digits with ****
        
        # Search and redact in all pages
        for page_num, page in enumerate(doc):
            # Initialize redacted regions for this page if not exists
            if page_num not in redacted_regions:
                redacted_regions[page_num] = []
            
            # Search for text instances (quads=False gives us Rect objects)
            text_instances = page.search_for(
                search_value,
                quads=False
            )
            
            for rect in text_instances:
                # Check if this rect overlaps with any already redacted region
                overlaps = False
                for redacted_rect in redacted_regions[page_num]:
                    if rectangles_overlap(rect, redacted_rect):
                        overlaps = True
                        print(f"⚠️ Skipping overlapping redaction at ({rect.x0:.1f}, {rect.y0:.1f})")
                        break
                
                if overlaps:
                    continue  # Skip this instance as it overlaps with existing redaction
                
                # Check if this rectangle likely spans multiple lines
                # by examining its height - typical single line is ~12-15 points
                rect_height = rect.y1 - rect.y0
                
                # If height suggests multiple lines (> 16 points), split by searching individual words
                # Lower threshold to catch more cases where redaction might bleed across lines
                if rect_height > 16:
                    # Get all words on the page
                    words = page.get_text("words")  # Returns list of (x0, y0, x1, y1, "word", block_no, line_no, word_no)
                    
                    # Find words that match our search term (case-insensitive)
                    matching_word_groups = []
                    search_words = original_value.lower().split()
                    
                    i = 0
                    while i < len(words):
                        # Check if this word starts a match
                        word_text = words[i][4].lower()
                        if search_words[0] in word_text or word_text in search_words[0]:
                            # Try to match the full phrase
                            group = [words[i]]
                            matched = len(search_words) == 1
                            
                            if len(search_words) > 1:
                                j = i + 1
                                word_idx = 1
                                while j < len(words) and word_idx < len(search_words):
                                    next_word = words[j][4].lower()
                                    if search_words[word_idx] in next_word or next_word in search_words[word_idx]:
                                        group.append(words[j])
                                        word_idx += 1
                                        if word_idx == len(search_words):
                                            matched = True
                                            break
                                    j += 1
                            
                            if matched and len(group) > 0:
                                matching_word_groups.append(group)
                                i += len(group)
                                continue
                        i += 1
                    
                    # Process each matched word group separately (ensures single-line redactions)
                    for word_group in matching_word_groups:
                        # Group words by line (using Y coordinate)
                        line_groups = []
                        current_line = [word_group[0]]
                        current_y = word_group[0][1]  # y0 coordinate
                        
                        for word in word_group[1:]:
                            word_y = word[1]
                            # If Y coordinate is close (within 3 pixels), same line
                            if abs(word_y - current_y) < 3:
                                current_line.append(word)
                            else:
                                # New line detected
                                if current_line:
                                    line_groups.append(current_line)
                                current_line = [word]
                                current_y = word_y
                        
                        # Add last line
                        if current_line:
                            line_groups.append(current_line)
                        
                        # Create separate redaction for each line
                        for line_idx, line_words in enumerate(line_groups):
                            # Build rectangle from line words
                            x0 = min(w[0] for w in line_words)
                            y0 = min(w[1] for w in line_words)
                            x1 = max(w[2] for w in line_words)
                            y1 = max(w[3] for w in line_words)
                            
                            line_rect = fitz.Rect(x0, y0, x1, y1 - 1)  # Shrink by 1px
                            
                            # Add redaction annotation with white fill only
                            page.add_redact_annot(line_rect, fill=(1, 1, 1))
                            
                            # Mark this specific line rectangle as redacted
                            redacted_regions[page_num].append(line_rect)
                            
                            # Only add replacement text on first line segment
                            if line_idx == 0:
                                text_insertions.append((page, line_rect, replacement))
                            
                            redaction_count += 1
                            pages_with_redactions.add(page_num)
                            
                            redaction_details.append({
                                "page": page_num + 1,
                                "type": entity_type,
                                "original": original_value,
                                "replacement": replacement,
                                "line_segment": line_idx + 1
                            })
                else:
                    # Single line redaction - process normally
                    # Shrink height by 1px to prevent bleeding into next line
                    rect_shrunk = fitz.Rect(rect.x0, rect.y0, rect.x1, rect.y1 - 1)
                    
                    # Add redaction annotation with white fill only (no text)
                    page.add_redact_annot(rect_shrunk, fill=(1, 1, 1))
                    
                    # Mark this specific rectangle as redacted
                    redacted_regions[page_num].append(rect_shrunk)
                    
                    # Store text insertion for later (after redactions applied)
                    text_insertions.append((page, rect_shrunk, replacement))
                    
                    redaction_count += 1
                    pages_with_redactions.add(page_num)
                    
                    redaction_details.append({
                        "page": page_num + 1,
                        "type": entity_type,
                        "original": original_value,
                        "replacement": replacement
                    })
    
    # Extract font information and original text length BEFORE applying redactions
    text_info_map = {}
    for page, rect, text in text_insertions:
        key = (id(page), rect.x0, rect.y0, rect.x1, rect.y1)
        
        # Extract original text length and font details from the rectangle area
        try:
            # Get original text length before it's redacted
            original_text = page.get_text("text", clip=rect).strip()
            original_length = len(original_text)
            
            # Extract font details
            blocks = page.get_text("dict", clip=rect)["blocks"]
            font_name = "helv"  # Default
            font_size = 11      # Default
            
            # Find first text span with font info
            for block in blocks:
                if "lines" in block:
                    for line in block["lines"]:
                        for span in line["spans"]:
                            if span.get("text", "").strip():
                                raw_font = span.get("font", "helv").lower()
                                font_size = span.get("size", 11)
                                
                                # Use simple, reliable font names
                                if "bold" in raw_font:
                                    font_name = "helv-bold"
                                elif "italic" in raw_font or "oblique" in raw_font:
                                    font_name = "helv-oblique"
                                elif "times" in raw_font:
                                    font_name = "times-roman"
                                elif "courier" in raw_font:
                                    font_name = "courier"
                                else:
                                    font_name = "helv"
                                break
                        if font_size != 11:
                            break
                    if font_size != 11:
                        break
            
            text_info_map[key] = (font_name, font_size, original_length)
            print(f"📝 Extracted: font={font_name}, size={font_size}, length={original_length}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Info extraction failed: {e}, using defaults")
            text_info_map[key] = ("helv", 11, len(text))
    
    # Apply redactions on pages that have them
    for page_num in pages_with_redactions:
        doc[page_num].apply_redactions()
    
    # Insert replacement text with matched font and normalized length
    for page, rect, text in text_insertions:
        key = (id(page), rect.x0, rect.y0, rect.x1, rect.y1)
        font_name, font_size, original_length = text_info_map.get(key, ("helv", 11, len(text)))
        
        # Smart length normalization based on replacement type
        replacement_text = text
        
        if len(text) != original_length:
            # Handle special cases for better formatting
            
            # Case 1: Bracketed replacements like [DIAGNOSIS], [TREATMENT], etc.
            if text.startswith('[') and text.endswith(']'):
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    # Pad inside the bracket: "[DIAGNOSIS]" -> "[DIAGNOSIS*****]"
                    replacement_text = text[:-1] + '*' * (original_length - len(text)) + ']'
                elif len(text) > original_length:
                    # Truncate but keep closing bracket: "[DIAGNOSIS]" -> "[DIAG]"
                    if original_length > 2:
                        replacement_text = text[:original_length - 1] + ']'
                    else:
                        replacement_text = text[:original_length]
            
            # Case 2: Account/Card numbers with **** prefix
            elif text.startswith('****'):
                # Pad beginning with more asterisks: "****1234" -> "********1234" for longer originals
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    replacement_text = '*' * (original_length - len(text)) + text
                else:
                    replacement_text = text[:original_length]
            
            # Case 3: SSN/TIN format (XXX-XX-1234 or XX-XXX)
            elif 'XXX-XX-' in text or 'XX-XXX' in text or text == 'XX-XXX':
                # These should already match length, but pad with * if needed
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    replacement_text = text + '*' * (original_length - len(text))
                else:
                    replacement_text = text[:original_length]
            
            # Case 4: Just a year (4 digits) - pad with asterisks for full date
            elif text.isdigit() and len(text) == 4:
                # Year only: pad with asterisks: "1985" -> "**/**/1985" or "****1985"
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    padding_needed = original_length - len(text)
                    # If original looks like a date format (10 chars like MM/DD/YYYY)
                    if original_length == 10:
                        replacement_text = '**/**/' + text
                    elif original_length == 8:
                        replacement_text = '****' + text
                    else:
                        replacement_text = '*' * padding_needed + text
                else:
                    replacement_text = text
            
            # Case 5: City, State format - pad with spaces
            elif ', ' in text and len(text.split(', ')) == 2:
                # Address like "Austin, TX" - pad with spaces
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    replacement_text = text + ' ' * (original_length - len(text))
                else:
                    replacement_text = text[:original_length]
            
            # Case 6: Initials format (A.B., J.S., etc.)
            # elif '.' in text and len(text) < 10:
            #     # Pad initials with spaces
            #     if len(text) < original_length:
            #         replacement_text = text + ' ' * (original_length - len(text))
            #     else:
            #         replacement_text = text[:original_length]
            
            # Default: Generic padding/truncation
            else:
                if len(text) < original_length:
                    # Pad with spaces
                    replacement_text = text + ' ' * (original_length - len(text))
                elif len(text) > original_length:
                    # Truncate
                    replacement_text = text[:original_length]
        
        # Insert text at proper baseline position
        try:
            # Calculate baseline position (20-25% from bottom)
            baseline_offset = font_size * 0.25
            y_pos = rect.y1 - baseline_offset
            x_pos = rect.x0 + 2
            
            # Reduce font size for uppercase redacted text to prevent width overflow
            # Use 0.7 for PROPRIETARY markers (wider text), 0.85 for others
            if '[PROP' in replacement_text:
                adjusted_font_size = font_size * 0.7
            else:
                adjusted_font_size = font_size * 0.85
            
            page.insert_text(
                (x_pos, y_pos),
                replacement_text,
                fontname=font_name,
                fontsize=adjusted_font_size,
                color=(0, 0, 0)
            )
            print(f"✅ Inserted '{replacement_text}' (len={len(replacement_text)}) with font={font_name}, size={adjusted_font_size:.1f}")
        except Exception as e:
            # Fallback to simple insertion if font fails
            print(f"⚠️ Text insertion failed with font '{font_name}': {e}, using helv")
            try:
                page.insert_text(
                    (rect.x0 + 2, rect.y1 - 2),
                    replacement_text,
                    fontname="helv",
                    fontsize=font_size * 0.85,
                    color=(0, 0, 0)
                )
                print(f"✅ Inserted '{replacement_text}' with fallback font helv")
            except Exception as e2:
                print(f"❌ Fallback insertion also failed: {e2}")
    
    # Save redacted document
    doc.save(output_path)
    
    return {
        "redaction_count": redaction_count,
        "entities_processed": len(entities),
        "details": redaction_details,
        "output_path": output_path
    }


def redact_pdf_document(
    input_pdf_path: str,
    output_pdf_path: str,
    return_metadata: bool = False
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Main function to redact PII/PHI from a PDF document.
    
    Args:
        input_pdf_path: Path to input PDF file
        output_pdf_path: Path where redacted PDF will be saved
        return_metadata: Whether to return detailed redaction metadata
    
    Returns:
        Dictionary with redaction results and metadata
    """
    try:
        # Initialize components
        client = get_azure_client()
        prompt_template = load_prompt_template()
        anonymizer = LegalAnonymizer()
        
        # Open PDF and extract text
        doc = fitz.open(input_pdf_path)
        text = extract_text_from_pdf(doc)
        page_count = doc.page_count  # Store before closing
        
        if not text.strip():
            doc.close()
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": "No text could be extracted from the PDF",
                "redaction_count": 0,
                "entities_processed": 0,
                "details": []
            }
        
        print(f"📄 Extracted {len(text)} characters from {page_count} pages")
        
        # Extract PII entities using AI
        entities = extract_pii_entities(client, text, prompt_template)
        print(f"🔍 Found {len(entities)} potential PII entities")
        
        # Apply redactions (deduplication happens inside)
        result = apply_redactions_to_pdf(
            doc,
            output_pdf_path,
            entities,
            anonymizer,
            case_sensitive=True
        )
        
        doc.close()
        
        # Return result based on return_metadata flag
        if return_metadata:
            return {
                "success": True,
                "redaction_count": result.get("redaction_count", 0),
                "entities_processed": result.get("entities_processed", 0),
                "details": result.get("details", []),
                "pages_processed": page_count,
                "entities_found": len(entities)
            }
        else:
            return {
                "success": True,
                "redaction_count": result.get("redaction_count", 0),
                "entities_processed": result.get("entities_processed", 0),
                "pages_processed": page_count
            }
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Error in redact_pdf_document: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        return {
            "success": False,
            "error": str(e),
            "error_type": type(e).__name__,
            "redaction_count": 0,
            "entities_processed": 0,
            "details": []
        }


def get_redaction_config() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Get PII redaction configuration and supported types."""
    return {
        "service": "Legal Document Redaction",
        "compliance": "FRCP 5.2",
        "supported_pii_types": [
            "Social Security Numbers (SSN)",
            "Tax Identification Numbers (TIN)",
            "Financial Account Numbers",
            "Passport Numbers",
            "Driver License Numbers",
            "Birth Dates",
            "Minor Names",
            "Home Addresses",
            "Medical Information",
            "Employment Information",
            "Financial Details",
            "Trade Secrets"
        ],
        "excluded_entities": [
            "Judge names",
            "Attorney names",
            "Case numbers",
            "Legal citations",
            "Court names"
        ],
        "formatting_rules": {
            "ssn": "XXX-XX-6789 (last 4 digits shown)",
            "dob": "DOB: 1985 (year only)",
            "minor_names": "E.S. (initials only)",
            "accounts": "Acct: ****4321 (last 4 digits)",
            "passport": "Pass: ****5678 (last 4 digits)",
            "drivers_license": "DL: ****4321 (last 4 digits)",
            "addresses": "City, State only",
            "medical": "[CONFIDENTIAL - MEDICAL INFORMATION]",
            "financial": "[REDACTED - FINANCIAL INFO]",
            "trade_secret": "[CONFIDENTIAL - TRADE SECRET]"
        }
    }


def pdf_to_base64_images(pdf_path: str, resolution_multiplier: float = 2.0) -> List[str]:
    """
    Convert PDF pages to base64 encoded PNG images for display.
    
    Args:
        pdf_path: Path to the PDF file
        resolution_multiplier: Scale factor for image resolution (default 2.0 for higher quality)
    
    Returns:
        List of base64-encoded PNG image strings
    """
    doc = fitz.open(pdf_path)
    images = []
    
    for page_num in range(len(doc)):
        page = doc[page_num]
        # Higher resolution for better quality
        pix = page.get_pixmap(
            matrix=fitz.Matrix(resolution_multiplier, resolution_multiplier)
        )
        img_data = pix.tobytes("png")
        img_base64 = base64.b64encode(img_data).decode()
        images.append(img_base64)
    
    doc.close()
    return images

export default function Home() {
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("redaction");
  const [showDashboardInstructions, setShowDashboardInstructions] = useState(false);
  const [apiHealthy, setApiHealthy] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Clear authentication state on first load to ensure login page shows
    // Only do this once when the app first loads
    const hasInitialized = sessionStorage.getItem('app_initialized');
    if (!hasInitialized) {
      localStorage.removeItem('isAuthenticated');
      sessionStorage.setItem('app_initialized', 'true');
    }
    
    // Check for saved authentication state after component mounts
    const checkAuth = () => {
      try {
        const savedAuth = localStorage.getItem('isAuthenticated');
        // Only set to authenticated if explicitly set to 'true'
        setIsAuthenticated(savedAuth === 'true');
        setIsLoading(false);
      } catch (error) {
        // In case localStorage is not available
        setIsAuthenticated(false);
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    
    // Small delay to ensure proper hydration
    setTimeout(checkAuth, 100);
    
    const checkApiHealth = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/health', {
          method: 'GET',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          },
        });
        setApiHealthy(response.ok);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('API health check failed:', error);
        setApiHealthy(false);
      }
    };

    checkApiHealth();
    const interval = setInterval(checkApiHealth, 60000); // Check every minute
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // Show loading state during hydration
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-50">
        <div className="text-lg text-slate-600">Loading...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Login onLogin={() => {
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
      localStorage.setItem('isAuthenticated', 'true');
    }} />;
  }

  return (
    <AnalysisProvider>
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      {/* Header */}

              />

              <div className="w-12 h-12 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Image
                  src="/Shield_logo2.svg"
                  alt="Shield Logo"
                  width={48}
                  height={48}
                  className="object-contain"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">

    </AnalysisProvider>

===== frontend\components\export-button.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from './ui/button';
import { Download, FileSpreadsheet, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import axios from 'axios';

interface ExportButtonProps {
  results: any[];
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function ExportButton({ results, disabled = false }: ExportButtonProps) {
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);

  const handleExport = async (format: 'excel' | 'csv') => {
    if (results.length === 0) {
      alert('No results to export');
      return;
    }

    setIsExporting(true);
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(
        `/api/export`,
        {
          format,
          results,
        },
        {
          responseType: 'blob',
        }
      );

      // Create download link
      const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([response.data]));
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.setAttribute(
        'download',
        `redaction_results.${format === 'excel' ? 'xlsx' : 'csv'}`
      );
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();
      window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Export error:', error);
      alert('Failed to export results. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2">
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        onClick={() => handleExport('excel')}
        disabled={disabled || isExporting || results.length === 0}
      >
        <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
        Export Excel
      </Button>
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        onClick={() => handleExport('csv')}
        disabled={disabled || isExporting || results.length === 0}
      >
        <FileText className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
        Export CSV
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

              <li className="hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer">AI PII Detection</li>

              <li className="hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer">HIPAA Protection</li>
              <li className="hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer">Document Security</li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          {/* System Information */}
          <div className="text-left">
            <h4 className="text-white font-semibold mb-5 text-base">System Information</h4>
            <ul className="space-y-3 text-sm text-slate-300">

===== frontend\components\login.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

interface LoginProps {
  onLogin: () => void;
}

export default function Login({ onLogin }: LoginProps) {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleLogin = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    // Simple auth check (replace with real authentication)
    if (username === "admin" && password === "password") {
      localStorage.setItem('isAuthenticated', 'true');
      onLogin();
    } else {
      setError("Invalid credentials. Try admin/password");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-gray-100">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md bg-white shadow-lg rounded-2xl border-0">
        <CardContent className="p-8">
          {/* Logo */}
          <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
            <div className="w-32 h-32 flex items-center justify-center">
              <img
                src="/Shield_logo2.svg"
                alt="Shield Logo"
                className="w-full h-full object-contain"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Title */}
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center mb-2 text-slate-800">PII/PHI Redaction Portal</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-8">
            AI-Powered Document Redaction with FRCP 5.2 and HIPAA Compliance
          </p>

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-5">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">Username</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border-2 border-gray-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#93d500] focus:border-transparent transition-all"
                placeholder="Enter your username"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">Password</label>
              <input
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border-2 border-gray-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#93d500] focus:border-transparent transition-all"
                placeholder="Enter your password"
              />
            </div>
            {error && (
              <div className="text-sm text-center text-red-600">{error}</div>
            )}
            <Button 
              type="submit" 
              className="w-full py-3 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg hover:bg-[#7ab800] transition-colors"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: '#93d500',
                border: 'none'
              }}
            >
              Login
            </Button>
          </form>

          {/* Demo Mode Info */}
          <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-[#93d500]">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">
              <span className="font-semibold">Demo Mode:</span> For prototype testing, use credentials{" "}
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-800 bg-gray-200 px-1 rounded">admin</span> /{" "}
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-800 bg-gray-200 px-1 rounded">password</span>
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>

}

===== frontend\components\pdf-viewer-modal.tsx =====
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Download, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';

interface PdfViewerModalProps {
  pdfUrl: string;
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function PdfViewerModal({ pdfUrl, title, onClose }: PdfViewerModalProps) {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const handleDownload = () => {
    // Simply open the URL - browser will handle download based on content-disposition
    window.open(pdfUrl, '_blank');
  };

  return (
    <div 
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/70"
      onClick={(e) => {
        // Only close if clicking directly on backdrop, not on children
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          onClose();
        }
      }}
    >
      <Card 
        className="w-full max-w-6xl h-[90vh] flex flex-col bg-white shadow-2xl relative pointer-events-auto"
      >
        <CardHeader className="flex-shrink-0 border-b relative z-50">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <CardTitle className="text-xl">{title}</CardTitle>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                onClose();
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-100 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden p-0 bg-gray-100 relative pointer-events-auto">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-white/80 z-10 pointer-events-none">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-blue-600" />
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Loading PDF document...</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <iframe
            src={`${pdfUrl}#view=FitH`}
            className="w-full h-full border-0 pointer-events-auto"
            title="PDF Viewer"
            onLoad={() => {
              setIsLoading(false);
            }}
            onError={(e) => {
              console.error('❌ PDF iframe error:', e);
              setIsLoading(false);
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend\components\pii-redaction.tsx =====
'use client';

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Upload, FileText, Download, AlertCircle, CheckCircle, ZoomIn, ZoomOut, Maximize, Shield, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, X, Loader2, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Toast } from './ui/toast';
import axios from 'axios';
import { useAnalysisContext } from '@/context/AnalysisContext';
import { API_URL } from '@/lib/api-config';

type RedactionState = 'idle' | 'uploading' | 'processing' | 'complete' | 'error';

interface RedactionMetadata {
  entities_found: number;
  pages_processed: number;
  entities: Array<{
    type: string;
    value: string;
    replacement?: string;
  }>;
  processing_time: number;
}

interface PDFPage {
  pageNumber: number;
  originalImage: string;
  redactedImage: string;
}

interface RedactionResult {
  fileName: string;
  metadata: RedactionMetadata;
  redactedFileUrl: string;
  pdfPages: PDFPage[];
  recordId?: string;
  isDuplicate?: boolean;
}

export default function PIIRedaction() {
  // Get persistent state from context
  const { 
    piiRedactionResults: results, 
    setPiiRedactionResults: setResults,
    piiCurrentPage: currentPage,
    setPiiCurrentPage: setCurrentPage,
    piiZoom: zoom,
    setPiiZoom: setZoom
  } = useAnalysisContext();
  
  // Local state (non-persistent)
  const [state, setState] = useState<RedactionState>('idle');
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showInstructions, setShowInstructions] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<{ message: string; type: 'success' | 'error' } | null>(null);
  const [duplicateBanner, setDuplicateBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingExample, setLoadingExample] = useState(false);
  
  const originalViewerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const redactedViewerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const isScrollingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    try {
      const selectedFiles = e.target.files;
      if (selectedFiles && selectedFiles.length > 0) {
        const pdfFiles = Array.from(selectedFiles).filter(file => {
          const isValidPDF = file.type === 'application/pdf' || file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf');
          const isValidSize = file.size <= 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50MB limit
          
          if (!isValidPDF) {
            console.warn(`Skipping non-PDF file: ${file.name}`);
          }
          if (!isValidSize) {
            console.warn(`Skipping large file: ${file.name} (${Math.round(file.size / 1024 / 1024)}MB)`);
          }
          
          return isValidPDF && isValidSize;
        });
        
        if (pdfFiles.length > 0) {
          setFiles(prev => [...prev, ...pdfFiles]);
          setError(null);
          
          if (pdfFiles.length !== selectedFiles.length) {
            setToastMessage({
              message: `Selected ${pdfFiles.length} valid PDF files (some files were skipped)`,
              type: 'success'
            });
          }
        } else {
          setError('Please select valid PDF files (maximum 50MB each)');
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error handling file selection:', err);
      setError('Error selecting files. Please try again.');
    }
  };

  const removeFile = (indexToRemove: number) => {
    setFiles(prev => prev.filter((_, index) => index !== indexToRemove));
  };

  const processDocuments = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (files.length === 0) return;
    if (state === 'processing') {
      console.warn('Processing already in progress');
      return;
    }

    setState('processing');
    setError(null);
    setToastMessage(null);

    try {
      const newResults: RedactionResult[] = [];
      const duplicateFiles: string[] = [];
      const failedFiles: string[] = [];
      
      // Create single FormData for all files (backend handles batch processing)
      const formData = new FormData();
      files.forEach(file => {
        formData.append('files', file);
      });
      formData.append('redaction_types', 'SSN,EMAIL,PHONE,NAME,ADDRESS,DOB,ACCOUNT,LICENSE');
      formData.append('preserve_format', 'true');
      
      console.log('Starting document processing...');
      
      const response = await axios.post(
        `${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/redact-with-metadata`,
        formData,
        {
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' },
          responseType: 'json',
          timeout: 600000, // 10 minutes timeout for batch processing
          withCredentials: false,
        }
      );

      console.log('Raw API Response:', response.data);

      // Backend returns: { session_id, total_files, results: [...] }
      const responseData = response.data;
      
      if (!responseData.results || !Array.isArray(responseData.results)) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid response structure from backend: ${JSON.stringify(responseData)}`);
      }

      // Process each result from the batch response
      for (const fileResult of responseData.results) {
        console.log('Processing file result:', fileResult);
        
        if (!fileResult.success) {
          console.error(`Processing failed for ${fileResult.file_name}:`, fileResult.error);
          const errorType = fileResult.error_type ? `${fileResult.error_type}: ` : '';
          failedFiles.push(`${fileResult.file_name}: ${errorType}${fileResult.error || 'Redaction failed'}`);
          continue;
        }

        // Build metadata from the actual response structure - handle both direct and nested entities
        let entityList = fileResult.entities || fileResult.entities_detail || [];
        console.log('Raw entity list for file:', fileResult.file_name, entityList);
        
        // Handle case where entities_detail is stored as JSON string in database
        if (typeof entityList === 'string') {
          try {
            entityList = JSON.parse(entityList);
            console.log('Parsed entities from JSON string:', entityList);
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Failed to parse entities JSON string:', e);
            entityList = [];
          }
        }
        
        const meta: RedactionMetadata = {
          entities_found: fileResult.entities_found || 0,
          pages_processed: fileResult.pages_processed || 0,
          processing_time: fileResult.processing_time || 0,
          entities: Array.isArray(entityList) ? entityList : []
        };
        
        const record_id: string = fileResult.redaction_id || '';
        const is_duplicate: boolean = false; // Backend handles duplicates internally

        // Only proceed if we have a valid record ID
        if (!record_id) {
          console.error(`Missing redaction_id for ${fileResult.file_name}`);
          continue;
        }

        // Create download URL for the redacted PDF
        const url = `${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${record_id}/download`;

        // Process page images from API response for side-by-side comparison
        const pdfPages: PDFPage[] = [];
        const originalImages = fileResult.original_images || [];
        const redactedImages = fileResult.redacted_images || [];
        
        // Create pages from the image arrays
        const maxPages = Math.max(originalImages.length, redactedImages.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < maxPages; i++) {
          pdfPages.push({
            pageNumber: i + 1,
            originalImage: originalImages[i] || '',
            redactedImage: redactedImages[i] || ''
          });
        }
        
        console.log(`Created ${pdfPages.length} page objects for ${fileResult.file_name}`);

        const newResult: RedactionResult = {
          fileName: fileResult.file_name || 'Unknown File',
          metadata: meta,
          redactedFileUrl: url,
          pdfPages: pdfPages,
          recordId: record_id,
          isDuplicate: is_duplicate,
        };

        newResults.push(newResult);
        console.log('Added result:', newResult);
      }

      if (newResults.length > 0) {
        setResults(newResults);
        setCurrentPage(0);
        setFiles([]); // Clear files after successful processing
        setState('complete');
        setError(null); // Clear any previous errors
        
        // Show success message
        setToastMessage({ 
          message: `Successfully processed ${newResults.length} document${newResults.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`, 
          type: 'success' 
        });

        if (failedFiles.length > 0) {
          setError(`Some files failed:\n${failedFiles.join('\n')}`);
          setToastMessage({
            message: `Processed ${newResults.length}, failed ${failedFiles.length}.`,
            type: 'error'
          });
        }
        
        // Clear duplicate banner since backend handles this
        setDuplicateBanner(null);
      } else {
        const details = failedFiles.length > 0 ? `\n\n${failedFiles.join('\n')}` : '';
        setError(`No documents were successfully processed.${details}`);
        setState('error');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Redaction error:', err);
      const errorMessage = err.response?.data?.detail || err.message || 'An error occurred during redaction';
      setError(errorMessage);
      setState('error');
      setToastMessage({ 
        message: `Error: ${errorMessage}`, 
        type: 'error' 
      });
    }
  };

  const base64ToBlob = (base64: string, mimeType: string): Blob => {
    const byteCharacters = atob(base64);
    const byteNumbers = new Array(byteCharacters.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < byteCharacters.length; i++) {
      byteNumbers[i] = byteCharacters.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    const byteArray = new Uint8Array(byteNumbers);
    return new Blob([byteArray], { type: mimeType });
  };

  const downloadAllFiles = async () => {
    if (results.length === 0) {
      setToastMessage({ message: 'No files to download', type: 'error' });
      return;
    }

    setToastMessage({ message: `Downloading ${results.length} file(s)...`, type: 'success' });

    for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
      const result = results[i];
      
      try {
        if (result.recordId) {
          const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${result.recordId}/download`);
          if (response.ok) {
            const blob = await response.blob();
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = url;
            link.download = `redacted_${result.fileName}.pdf`;
            document.body.appendChild(link);
            link.click();
            document.body.removeChild(link);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
          }
        } else if (result.redactedFileUrl) {
          const link = document.createElement('a');
          link.href = result.redactedFileUrl;
          link.download = `redacted_${result.fileName}.pdf`;
          document.body.appendChild(link);
          link.click();
          document.body.removeChild(link);
        }
        // Small delay between downloads to prevent browser blocking
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 300));
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Error downloading ${result.fileName}:`, err);
      }
    }

    setToastMessage({ message: `Downloaded ${results.length} file(s) successfully`, type: 'success' });
  };

  const downloadFile = async () => {
    if (results.length > 0 && currentPage < results.length) {
      const currentResult = results[currentPage];
      
      if (!currentResult) {
        setError('No valid result found for download');
        return;
      }
      
      // If we have a record ID, download via API
      if (currentResult.recordId) {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${currentResult.recordId}/download`);
          if (response.ok) {
            const blob = await response.blob();
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = url;
            link.download = `redacted_${currentResult.fileName}.pdf`;
            document.body.appendChild(link);
            link.click();
            document.body.removeChild(link);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            
            setToastMessage({ 
              message: 'File downloaded successfully', 
              type: 'success' 
            });
          } else {
            console.error('Failed to download file:', response.statusText);
            setToastMessage({ 
              message: 'Failed to download file. Please check if the file exists in storage.', 
              type: 'error' 
            });
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Download error:', err);
          setToastMessage({ 
            message: 'Error downloading file. Please try again.', 
            type: 'error' 
          });
        }
      } else if (currentResult.redactedFileUrl) {
        // Fallback for blob URLs
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = currentResult.redactedFileUrl;
        link.download = `redacted_${currentResult.fileName}.pdf`;
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);
        
        setToastMessage({ 
          message: 'File downloaded successfully', 
          type: 'success' 
        });
      } else {
        setToastMessage({ 
          message: 'No file available for download', 
          type: 'error' 
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const resetForm = () => {
    setState('idle');
    setFiles([]);
    
    // Revoke blob URLs only (not API endpoints)
    results.forEach(result => {
      if (result.redactedFileUrl && result.redactedFileUrl.startsWith('blob:')) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(result.redactedFileUrl);
      }
    });
    
    setResults([]);
    setCurrentPage(0);
    setZoom(1.0);
    setError(null);
    setDuplicateBanner(null);
    setToastMessage(null);
