    // Clear file input
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleZoom = (factor: number) => {
    setZoom(prev => Math.max(0.5, Math.min(3.0, prev * factor)));
  };

  const resetZoom = () => {
    setZoom(1.0);
  };

  // Sync scroll between original and redacted viewers
  useEffect(() => {
    const originalViewer = originalViewerRef.current;
    const redactedViewer = redactedViewerRef.current;

    if (!originalViewer || !redactedViewer) return;

    const syncScroll = (source: HTMLDivElement, target: HTMLDivElement) => {
      if (!isScrollingRef.current) {
        isScrollingRef.current = true;
        target.scrollTop = source.scrollTop;
        target.scrollLeft = source.scrollLeft;
        setTimeout(() => { isScrollingRef.current = false; }, 50);
      }
    };

    const handleOriginalScroll = () => syncScroll(originalViewer, redactedViewer);
    const handleRedactedScroll = () => syncScroll(redactedViewer, originalViewer);

    originalViewer.addEventListener('scroll', handleOriginalScroll);
    redactedViewer.addEventListener('scroll', handleRedactedScroll);

    return () => {
      originalViewer.removeEventListener('scroll', handleOriginalScroll);
      redactedViewer.removeEventListener('scroll', handleRedactedScroll);
    };
  }, [results, currentPage]);

  // Keyboard shortcuts for zoom
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
        if (e.key === '=' || e.key === '+') {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleZoom(1.2);
        } else if (e.key === '-') {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleZoom(0.8);
        } else if (e.key === '0') {
          e.preventDefault();
          resetZoom();
        }
      }
    };

    if (state === 'complete') {
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    }
  }, [state]);

  // Reset zoom when changing pages
  useEffect(() => {
    resetZoom();
  }, [currentPage]);

  // Set state to complete when returning to tab with existing results
  useEffect(() => {
    if (results.length > 0 && state === 'idle') {
      setState('complete');
    }
  }, [results, state]);

  const currentResult: RedactionResult | null = results.length > 0 && currentPage < results.length ? results[currentPage] : null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Instructions Card */}
      <Card className="mb-6 bg-white" style={{ border: '2px solid #58bdcc' }}>
        <CardHeader className="cursor-pointer" onClick={() => setShowInstructions(!showInstructions)}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <CardTitle className="text-xl flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-900">
                📖 How to Use: PII/PHI Redaction
                <span className="text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">(Click to {showInstructions ? 'collapse' : 'expand'})</span>
              </CardTitle>
              <CardDescription className="text-slate-600">
                Upload PDF documents for automated PII/PHI detection and redaction
              </CardDescription>
            </div>
            {showInstructions ? (
              <ChevronUp className="w-6 h-6" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} />
            ) : (
              <ChevronDown className="w-6 h-6" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} />
            )}
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        {showInstructions && (
          <CardContent className="space-y-4 text-slate-700">
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2 text-slate-900">Overview</h4>
              <p className="text-sm">
                This tool automatically detects and redacts personally identifiable information (PII) and protected health information (PHI) from PDF documents, 
                following FRCP 5.2 compliance standards for legal court documents.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2 text-slate-900">Steps:</h4>
              <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-2 text-sm">
                <li>Upload one or more PDF files using the upload area below</li>
                <li>Click "Process Documents" to begin processing all files</li>
                <li>Review the side-by-side comparison of original vs redacted document</li>
                <li>Check the detected entities list to see what was redacted</li>
                <li>Use zoom controls to inspect details and Previous/Next buttons to navigate between documents</li>
                <li><strong>Approve or Reject:</strong> Click the green "Approve" button to accept the redaction, or the grey "Reject" button to decline (with required rejection notes)</li>
                <li>After approve/reject, the system automatically advances to the next document (if multiple files uploaded)</li>
                <li>Download individual redacted PDFs or review all redactions in the Dashboard tab</li>
              </ol>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-lg p-3 bg-cyan-50 border" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
              <p className="text-sm">
                <strong>Note:</strong> The system identifies and redacts sensitive information including Social Security Numbers, 
                dates of birth, minor names, financial account numbers, passport numbers, driver's license numbers, addresses, 
                medical information, and proprietary business data to ensure document confidentiality.
              </p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        )}
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-lg border-2" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-900">
            <Shield className="w-7 h-7" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} />
            PII/PHI Document Redaction
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription className="text-slate-600">
            FRCP 5.2 compliant redaction for legal documents
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
          
        <CardContent>
          {(state === 'idle' || (state === 'complete' && files.length > 0)) && (
            <div>
              <div
                className="border-3 border-dashed rounded-lg p-12 text-center"
                style={{ 
                  borderColor: '#58bdcc',
                  backgroundColor: '#f0f9fb'
                }}
              >
                <Upload className="mx-auto" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} size={64} />
                <h3 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold text-gray-700">
                  Upload PDF Documents
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600 mb-4">
                  Select one or multiple PDF files
                </p>
                <input
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  type="file"
                  accept=".pdf,application/pdf"
                  multiple
                  onChange={handleFileChange}
                  className="hidden"
                  id="pdf-file-input"
                />
                <Button
                  onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                  className="text-white"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                >
                  <Upload className="mr-2" size={20} />
                  Choose PDF Files
                </Button>
              </div>

              {/* Example Data Button */}
              <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 mb-3">
                  📚 Don't have test data? Try this example:
                </p>
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    try {
                      setLoadingExample(true);
                      const filename = 'Court_Filing_01.pdf';
                      const response = await fetch(`/${filename}`);
                      if (!response.ok) {
                        throw new Error(`Failed to fetch: ${response.status}`);
                      }
                      const blob = await response.blob();
                      const file = new File([blob], filename, { type: 'application/pdf' });
                      setFiles(prev => {
                        // Avoid adding duplicate example files
                        const isDuplicate = prev.some(
                          existing =>
                            existing.name === file.name &&
                            existing.size === file.size &&
                            existing.type === file.type
                        );
                        if (isDuplicate) {
                          return prev;
                        }

                        // Basic total size guard to mirror upload limits
                        const MAX_TOTAL_SIZE_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50 MB
                        const totalSize =
                          prev.reduce((sum, existing) => sum + existing.size, 0) + file.size;
                        if (totalSize > MAX_TOTAL_SIZE_BYTES) {
                          setToastMessage({
                            message: 'Total file size limit exceeded. Example document was not added.',
                            type: 'error',
                          });
                          return prev;
                        }

                        return [...prev, file];
                      });
                      setToastMessage({ message: 'Example court filing document loaded!', type: 'success' });
                    } catch (error) {
                      console.error('Failed to load example:', error);
                      setToastMessage({ message: 'Failed to load example. Check console for details.', type: 'error' });
                    } finally {
                      setLoadingExample(false);
                    }
                  }}
                  disabled={loadingExample}
                  className="w-full"
                  style={{ borderColor: '#03667a', color: loadingExample ? '#8c8c8c' : '#03667a' }}
                >
                  {loadingExample ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                      Loading...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    'Load Example PII/PHI Document'
                  )}
                </Button>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">
                  This is a sample court filing document with PII/PHI. Use it to test the redaction without uploading your own files.
                </p>
              </div>

              {files.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                      Selected files ({files.length}):
                    </p>
                    <Button
                      variant="ghost"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => setFiles([])}
                      className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50"
                    >
                      Clear All
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    {files.map((file, index) => (
                      <div
                        key={index}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 rounded px-3 py-2 group hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
                      >
                        <FileText className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
                        <span className="flex-1 truncate">{file.name}</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-400 flex-shrink-0">
                          ({(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)
                        </span>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => removeFile(index)}
                          className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-red-100 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 transition-colors opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                          title="Remove file"
                        >
                          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <Button
                    onClick={processDocuments}
                    disabled={state === 'processing'}
                    className="w-full mt-4 text-white"
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor: state === 'processing' ? '#8c8c8c' : '#58bdcc',
                      border: 'none'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                      if (state !== 'processing') {
                        e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a';
                      }
                    }}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                      if (state !== 'processing') {
                        e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc';
                      }
                    }}
                  >
                    {state === 'processing' ? (
                      <>
                        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                        Processing...
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      '🔒 Process Documents'
                    )}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {state === 'processing' && (
            <div className="text-center py-12">
              <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-16 w-16 border-b-4 mx-auto" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}></div>
              <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-medium text-gray-700">Processing documents...</p>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Analyzing and redacting PHI/PII entities from {files.length} file(s)</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {state === 'complete' && currentResult && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-center mb-4">
                <CheckCircle className="inline-block" style={{ color: '#93d500' }} size={40} />
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 mt-1">Redaction Complete!</h2>
                <p className="text-gray-600">
                  {currentResult.fileName}
                </p>
                {results.length > 1 && (
                  <div className="mt-3">
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-2">
                      Viewing page {currentPage + 1} of {results.length}
                    </p>
                    {/* Pagination Controls at Top */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.max(0, p - 1))}
                        disabled={currentPage === 0}
                      >
                        <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4 mr-1" />
                        Previous
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.min(results.length - 1, p + 1))}
                        disabled={currentPage === results.length - 1}
                      >
                        Next
                        <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-1" />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Duplicate Detection Banner */}
              {duplicateBanner && (
                <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-yellow-500 bg-yellow-50 flex items-start gap-3">
                  <AlertCircle className="text-yellow-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={24} />
                  <p className="text-yellow-800 font-medium">{duplicateBanner}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
                <Card className="text-white" style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="text-3xl font-bold">{currentResult.metadata.entities_found}</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-sm">PHI/PII Entities Found</div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
                <Card className="text-white" style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="text-3xl font-bold">{currentResult.metadata.pages_processed}</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-sm">Page(s) Processed</div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              </div>

              {currentResult.metadata.entities && currentResult.metadata.entities.length > 0 && (
                <Card className="mb-6">
                  <CardHeader className="py-3">
                    <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-900">
                      <FileText size={18} style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} />
                      Detected PHI/PII Entities
                    </CardTitle>
                  </CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <div className="max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50 rounded-lg p-4">
                      {(() => {
                        console.log('Current entity structure:', currentResult.metadata.entities);
                        return currentResult.metadata.entities.map((entity, idx) => (
                          <div key={idx} className="bg-white border-l-4 p-3 mb-2 rounded shadow" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                            <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: '#03667a' }}>
                              {entity.type || 'Unknown Type'}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-gray-600 font-mono text-sm bg-gray-100 inline-block px-2 py-1 rounded mt-1">
                              Original: {entity.original || entity.value || 'Not available'}
                            </div>
                            {entity.replacement && (
                              <div className="text-blue-600 font-mono text-sm bg-blue-50 inline-block px-2 py-1 rounded mt-1 ml-2">
                                Replaced with: {entity.replacement}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        ));
                      })()}
                    </div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}

              {currentResult.pdfPages.length > 0 ? (
                <>
                  {/* Zoom Controls */}
                  <div className="text-center mb-4 bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg border" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                    <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-4">
                      <Button
                        onClick={() => handleZoom(1.2)}
                        className="text-white"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                        title="Zoom In (Ctrl + +)"
                      >
                        <ZoomIn size={16} className="mr-2" />
                        Zoom In
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        onClick={() => handleZoom(0.8)}
                        className="text-white"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                        title="Zoom Out (Ctrl + -)"
                      >
                        <ZoomOut size={16} className="mr-2" />
                        Zoom Out
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        onClick={resetZoom}
                        className="text-white"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                        title="Reset Zoom (Ctrl + 0)"
                      >
                        <Maximize size={16} className="mr-2" />
                        Reset Zoom
                      </Button>
                      <span className="font-bold text-lg border-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-white" style={{ color: '#03667a', borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                        Zoom: {Math.round(zoom * 100)}%
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                      <FileText size={12} />
                      Keyboard shortcuts: Ctrl + Plus/Minus to zoom, Ctrl + 0 to reset
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Side-by-side comparison */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-6">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-white p-3 rounded-t-lg text-center font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2" style={{ backgroundColor: '#6b7280' }}>
                        <FileText size={20} />
                        Original Document
                      </div>
                      <div 
                        ref={originalViewerRef}
                        className="border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-b-lg bg-gray-100 p-4 h-[600px] overflow-y-auto"
                      >
                        {currentResult.pdfPages.map((page) => (
                          <div key={`original-${page.pageNumber}`} className="mb-4 text-center">
                            <img
                              src={`data:image/png;base64,${page.originalImage}`}
                              alt={`Original Page ${page.pageNumber}`}
                              className="max-w-full border border-gray-400 rounded shadow-lg mx-auto"
                              style={{ transform: `scale(${zoom})`, transformOrigin: 'top center' }}
                            />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <div className="text-white p-3 rounded-t-lg text-center font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2" style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                        <Shield size={20} />
                        Redacted Document
                      </div>
                      <div 
                        ref={redactedViewerRef}
                        className="border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-b-lg bg-gray-100 p-4 h-[600px] overflow-y-auto"
                      >
                        {currentResult.pdfPages.map((page) => (
                          <div key={`redacted-${page.pageNumber}`} className="mb-4 text-center">
                            <img
                              src={`data:image/png;base64,${page.redactedImage}`}
                              alt={`Redacted Page ${page.pageNumber}`}
                              className="max-w-full border border-gray-400 rounded shadow-lg mx-auto"
                              style={{ transform: `scale(${zoom})`, transformOrigin: 'top center' }}
                            />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="mb-6 p-6 rounded-lg border-2 text-center" style={{ backgroundColor: '#f0f9fb', borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                  <AlertCircle className="mx-auto mb-4" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} size={48} />
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2" style={{ color: '#03667a' }}>Document Processing Complete</h3>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 mb-4">
                    The document has been successfully processed and redacted. The side-by-side visual comparison 
                    is not available in this version, but you can download the redacted PDF to review the changes.
                  </p>
                  <div className="bg-white rounded-lg p-4 border" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700">
                      <strong>Processing Summary:</strong><br />
                      • {currentResult.metadata.entities_found} PII/PHI entities detected and redacted<br />
                      • {currentResult.metadata.pages_processed} page(s) processed<br />
                      • Processing completed in {Math.round(currentResult.metadata.processing_time * 100) / 100} seconds
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Pagination Controls */}
              {results.length > 1 && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mb-6 pb-6 border-b">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.max(0, p - 1))}
                    disabled={currentPage === 0}
                  >
                    <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4 mr-1" />
                    Previous
                  </Button>
                  <span className="text-sm text-slate-600">
                    Page {currentPage + 1} of {results.length}
                  </span>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.min(results.length - 1, p + 1))}
                    disabled={currentPage === results.length - 1}
                  >
                    Next
                    <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-1" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Approve/Reject Buttons */}
              {currentResult.recordId && (
                <div className="flex gap-4 mb-6">
                  <Button
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (!currentResult.recordId) {
                        setToastMessage({ message: 'Cannot approve: Record ID not found', type: 'error' });
                        return;
                      }
                      try {
                        const response = await fetch(
                          `${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${currentResult.recordId}/review?review_status=approved&reviewed_by=admin`,
                          { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
                        );
                        if (response.ok) {
                          setToastMessage({ message: 'Redaction approved for: ' + currentResult.fileName, type: 'success' });
                          
                          // Auto-navigate to next document
                          if (currentPage < results.length - 1) {
                            setCurrentPage(currentPage + 1);
                          }
                        } else {
                          const errorText = await response.text();
                          console.error('Failed to approve redaction:', errorText);
                          setToastMessage({ message: 'Failed to approve redaction', type: 'error' });
                        }
                      } catch (error) {
                        console.error('Error approving redaction:', error);
                        setToastMessage({ message: 'Error approving redaction', type: 'error' });
                      }
                    }}
                    className="flex-1 text-white"
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor: '#93d500',
                      border: 'none'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#7ab800';
                    }}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#93d500';
                    }}
                  >
                    <CheckCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Approve
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (!currentResult.recordId) {
                        setToastMessage({ message: 'Cannot reject: Record ID not found', type: 'error' });
                        return;
                      }
                      const reason = prompt('Enter reason for rejection:');
                      if (reason) {
                        try {
                          const response = await fetch(
                            `${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${currentResult.recordId}/review?review_status=rejected&reviewed_by=admin&review_notes=${encodeURIComponent(reason)}`,
                            { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
                          );
                          if (response.ok) {
                            setToastMessage({ message: 'Redaction rejected for: ' + currentResult.fileName, type: 'success' });
                            
                            // Auto-navigate to next document
                            if (currentPage < results.length - 1) {
                              setCurrentPage(currentPage + 1);
                            }
                          } else {
                            const errorText = await response.text();
                            console.error('Failed to reject redaction:', errorText);
                            setToastMessage({ message: 'Failed to reject redaction', type: 'error' });
                          }
                        } catch (error) {
                          console.error('Error rejecting redaction:', error);
                          setToastMessage({ message: 'Error rejecting redaction', type: 'error' });
                        }
                      }
                    }}
                    variant="destructive"
                    className="flex-1"
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor: '#565656',
                      color: '#ffffff',
                      border: 'none'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#8c8c8c';
                    }}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#565656';
                    }}
                  >
                    <XCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Reject
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex justify-center gap-4">
                <Button
                  onClick={downloadFile}
                  className="text-white"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                >
                  <Download className="mr-2" size={20} />
                  Download Redacted PDF
                </Button>
                {results.length > 1 && (
                  <Button
                    onClick={downloadAllFiles}
                    className="text-white"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: '#58bdcc' }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#58bdcc'}
                  >
                    <Download className="mr-2" size={20} />
                    Download All ({results.length})
                  </Button>
                )}
                <Button
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  className="text-white"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: '#088c87' }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#03667a'}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#088c87'}
                >
                  <Upload className="mr-2" size={20} />
                  Process More Documents
                </Button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 p-4 rounded-lg flex items-start border-2" style={{ backgroundColor: '#f0f9fb', borderColor: '#58bdcc' }}>
                <AlertCircle className="mr-2 mt-1 flex-shrink-0" style={{ color: '#03667a' }} size={20} />
                <div className="text-slate-700">
                  <strong style={{ color: '#03667a' }}>Storage Policy:</strong> Only redacted PDFs are saved to secure storage. Original files are deleted for your security and privacy.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center">
              <AlertCircle className="text-red-600 mr-2" size={24} />
              <span className="text-red-700">{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Toast Notification */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <Toast
          message={toastMessage.message}
          type={toastMessage.type}
          duration={3000}
          onClose={() => setToastMessage(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend\components\pii-review-table.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { 
  CheckCircle, 
  XCircle,
  Eye,
  Download,
  FileText,
  Search,
  Filter,
  RefreshCw,
  RotateCcw,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Calendar,
  X,
  Trash2
} from "lucide-react";
import { PdfViewerModal } from "./pdf-viewer-modal";
import { API_URL } from "@/lib/api-config";

// Placeholder data structure
interface PIIRedaction {
  id: string;
  file_name: string;
  redacted_at: string;
  entities_found: number;
  pages_processed: number;
  processing_time_seconds?: number;
  review_status: 'needs_review' | 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'needs_revision';
  reviewed_by?: string;
  reviewed_at?: string;
  review_notes?: string;
}

export default function PIIReviewTable() {
  const [redactions, setRedactions] = useState<PIIRedaction[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>("all");
  const [filterDate, setFilterDate] = useState<string>("");
  const [autoRefresh, setAutoRefresh] = useState(true);
  const [lastRefresh, setLastRefresh] = useState<Date>(new Date());
  const [actionInProgress, setActionInProgress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pdfViewerOpen, setPdfViewerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pdfViewerUrl, setPdfViewerUrl] = useState("");

  // Handle view PDF
  const handleViewPdf = async (redactionId: string) => {
    try {
      // First check if the document exists
      const checkResponse = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${redactionId}`);
      if (!checkResponse.ok) {
        alert('Document not found or not accessible.');
        return;
      }
      
      const recordData = await checkResponse.json();
      if (!recordData.redacted_blob_name) {
        alert('Redacted document not available in storage. The file may not have been processed correctly.');
        return;
      }

      const url = `${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${redactionId}/view`;
      setPdfViewerUrl(url);
      setPdfViewerOpen(true);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error checking document:', error);
      alert('Error accessing document. Please try again.');
    }
  };

  // Handle delete
  const handleDelete = async (redactionId: string, fileName: string) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete "${fileName}"? This action cannot be undone.`)) {
      return;
    }
    
    setActionInProgress(redactionId);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${redactionId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
      });

      if (response.ok) {
        // Refresh data after successful deletion
        await fetchData();
      } else {
        console.error('Failed to delete redaction:', response.statusText);
        alert('Failed to delete redaction. Please try again.');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error deleting redaction:', error);
      alert('Error deleting redaction. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setActionInProgress(null);
    }
  };

  // Handle review actions
  const handleReviewAction = async (
    redactionId: string, 
    action: 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'needs_revision',
    notes?: string
  ) => {
    setActionInProgress(redactionId);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${redactionId}/review?review_status=${action}&reviewed_by=admin${notes ? `&review_notes=${encodeURIComponent(notes)}` : ''}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
      });

      if (response.ok) {
        // Refresh data after successful action
        await fetchData();
      } else {
        console.error('Failed to update review status:', response.statusText);
        alert('Failed to update review status. Please try again.');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error updating review status:', error);
      alert('Error updating review status. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setActionInProgress(null);
    }
  };

  // Fetch data from API
  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (filterStatus !== "all") {
        params.append("status", filterStatus);
      }
      params.append("limit", "100");
      
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/list?${params.toString()}`);
      if (response.ok) {
        const result = await response.json();
        let fetchedData = result.records || result.data || [];
        
        // Apply client-side date filtering - show redactions on or after the selected date
        if (filterDate) {
          const selectedDate = new Date(filterDate);
          selectedDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); // Start of selected day
          
          fetchedData = fetchedData.filter((item: PIIRedaction) => {
            const itemDate = new Date(item.redacted_at);
            itemDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); // Start of item day
            return itemDate >= selectedDate;
          });
        }
        
        setRedactions(fetchedData);
      } else {
        console.error("Failed to fetch PII redactions:", response.status, response.statusText);
        // Don't clear existing data on error to prevent flickering
        if (redactions.length === 0) {
          setRedactions([]);
        }
      }
      setLastRefresh(new Date());
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error fetching PII redaction data:", error);
      // Don't clear existing data on error to prevent flickering
      if (redactions.length === 0) {
        setRedactions([]);
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [filterStatus, filterDate]);  // Removed redactions from dependencies to prevent infinite loops

  // Load data on mount and when filters change
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData();
  }, [fetchData]);

  // Auto-refresh every 10 seconds when enabled - use stable reference
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!autoRefresh) return;

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      fetchData();
    }, 10000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [autoRefresh, fetchData]);

  // Filter data
  const filteredData = redactions.filter((item) => {
    const matchesSearch = item.file_name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = filterStatus === "all" || item.review_status === filterStatus;
    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  // Calculate statistics
  const totalRedactions = redactions.length;
  const pendingReview = redactions.filter((item) => item.review_status === "needs_review").length;
  const approved = redactions.filter((item) => item.review_status === "approved").length;
  const rejected = redactions.filter((item) => item.review_status === "rejected").length;
  const reviewed = approved + rejected;
  const approvalRate = reviewed > 0 ? ((approved / reviewed) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0";
  
  const redactionsWithTime = redactions.filter((item) => item.processing_time_seconds != null && item.processing_time_seconds > 0);
  const avgRedactionTime = redactionsWithTime.length > 0
    ? redactionsWithTime.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.processing_time_seconds || 0), 0) / redactionsWithTime.length
    : 0;

  const getStatusBadge = (status: PIIRedaction['review_status']) => {
    const styles = {
      needs_review: { backgroundColor: '#f59e0b', color: '#ffffff' },
      approved: { backgroundColor: '#93d500', color: '#ffffff' },
      rejected: { backgroundColor: '#565656', color: '#ffffff' },
      needs_revision: { backgroundColor: '#f97316', color: '#ffffff' },
    };

    const labels = {
      needs_review: 'Needs Review',
      approved: 'Approved',
      rejected: 'Rejected',
      needs_revision: 'Needs Revision',
    };

    return (
      <Badge
        className="text-white"
        style={styles[status] || { backgroundColor: '#6b7280', color: '#ffffff' }}
      >
        {labels[status] || status}
      </Badge>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Auto-refresh Status Indicator */}
      {autoRefresh && (
        <div className="rounded-lg px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between" style={{ backgroundColor: '#c8e3eb', borderColor: '#58bdcc', border: '1px solid' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" style={{ animationDuration: '3s', color: '#03667a' }} />
            <span className="text-sm" style={{ color: '#03667a' }}>
              Auto-refresh enabled • Updates every 10 seconds
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs" style={{ color: '#03667a' }}>
            Last updated: {lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString()}
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Statistics Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        {/* Total Redactions - Lightest */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border-2 hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow" style={{ borderColor: '#c8e3eb', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff 0%, #c8e3eb 100%)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 mb-2">Total Documents Redacted</p>
              <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">{totalRedactions}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">Complete redactions</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(200, 227, 235, 0.4)' }}>
              <FileText className="w-8 h-8" style={{ color: '#c8e3eb' }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Pending Review - Light */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border-2 hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow" style={{ borderColor: '#58bdcc', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff 0%, rgba(88, 189, 204, 0.3) 100%)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 mb-2">Pending Review</p>
              <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">{pendingReview}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">Awaiting verification</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(88, 189, 204, 0.25)' }}>
              <svg className="w-8 h-8" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Average Entities - Medium */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border-2 hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow" style={{ borderColor: '#088c87', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff 0%, rgba(8, 140, 135, 0.25) 100%)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 mb-2">Avg Redaction Time</p>
              <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                {avgRedactionTime > 0 ? `${avgRedactionTime.toFixed(1)}s` : '—'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">Per document processed</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(8, 140, 135, 0.25)' }}>
              <svg className="w-8 h-8" style={{ color: '#088c87' }} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Approval Rate - Darkest */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border-2 hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow" style={{ borderColor: '#03667a', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff 0%, rgba(3, 102, 122, 0.3) 100%)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 mb-2">Approval Rate</p>
              <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">{approvalRate}%</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">
                {approved} approved, {rejected} rejected
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(3, 102, 122, 0.25)' }}>
              <svg className="w-8 h-8" style={{ color: '#03667a' }} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-2a2 2 0 01-2-2z" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Review Progress Section */}
      {totalRedactions > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-lg border p-6" style={{ borderColor: '#c8e3eb' }}>
          <div className="mb-4">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <svg className="w-5 h-5" style={{ color: '#03667a' }} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-2a2 2 0 01-2-2z" />
              </svg>
              Review Progress
            </h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-1">Track PII redaction review completion status</p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-2">
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">Overall Progress</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  {reviewed} of {totalRedactions} reviewed ({((reviewed / totalRedactions) * 100).toFixed(0)}%)
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full bg-slate-200 rounded-full h-4 overflow-hidden shadow-inner">
                <div className="h-full flex">
                  <div 
                    className="transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{ 
                      width: `${(approved / totalRedactions * 100).toFixed(1)}%`,
                      backgroundColor: '#93d500'
                    }}
                  />
                  <div 
                    className="transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{ 
                      width: `${(rejected / totalRedactions * 100).toFixed(1)}%`,
                      backgroundColor: '#565656'
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mt-3 text-slate-600">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: '#93d500' }}></div>
                  <span>{approved} Approved</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: '#565656' }}></div>
                  <span>{rejected} Rejected</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: '#f59e0b' }}></div>
                  <span>{pendingReview} Needs Review</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {reviewed > 0 && (
              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div className="bg-green-50 rounded-lg p-4 border" style={{ borderColor: '#93d500' }}>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">Approved Redactions</p>
                    <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-2" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }}>{approved}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 mt-1">
                      {totalRedactions > 0 ? ((approved / totalRedactions) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0'}% of total
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-lg p-4 border" style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa', borderColor: '#565656' }}>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">Rejected Redactions</p>
                    <p className="text-3xl font-bold mt-2" style={{ color: '#565656' }}>{rejected}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 mt-1">
                      {totalRedactions > 0 ? ((rejected / totalRedactions) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0'}% of total
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Filters and Search */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4">
          <div className="flex-1 relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" />
            <Input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search by filename..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10"
            />
          </div>
          
          <Select value={filterStatus} onValueChange={setFilterStatus}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-[180px]">
              <Filter className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              <SelectValue placeholder="Status" />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="all">All Statuses</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="needs_review">Needs Review</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="approved">Approved</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="rejected">Rejected</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="needs_revision">Needs Revision</SelectItem>
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          <Button
            onClick={fetchData}
            disabled={loading}
            variant="outline"
            className="w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 mr-2 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            Refresh
          </Button>

          <Button
            onClick={() => setAutoRefresh(!autoRefresh)}
            variant={autoRefresh ? "default" : "outline"}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto"
            style={autoRefresh ? { backgroundColor: '#58bdcc', color: '#ffffff' } : {}}
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Auto-Refresh {autoRefresh ? 'On' : 'Off'}
          </Button>
        </div>

        {/* Date Filter Row */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3">
          <Calendar className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-600" />
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Analysis Date (On or After):</span>
          <Input
            type="date"
            value={filterDate}
            onChange={(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setFilterDate(e.target.value)}
            className="w-[180px]"
          />
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => {
              const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
              setFilterDate(today);
            }}
            className="bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 border-blue-200"
          >
            Today
          </Button>
          {filterDate && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => setFilterDate("")}
              className="text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
              Clear
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div className="border rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow className="bg-gray-50">
              <TableHead className="font-semibold">File Name</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold">Analysis Date</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold">Status</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold">Review Notes</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold">Actions</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {filteredData.length === 0 ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={5} className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500">
                  {loading ? 'Loading...' : 'No redactions found. Upload and redact documents to begin.'}
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              filteredData.map((redaction) => (
                <TableRow 
                  key={redaction.id} 
                  className="hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer"
                  onClick={() => handleViewPdf(redaction.id)}
                >
                  <TableCell>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <FileText className="h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#58bdcc' }} />
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{redaction.file_name}</span>
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                    <div className="flex flex-col">
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">
                        {new Date(redaction.redacted_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { 
                          month: 'short', 
                          day: 'numeric',
                          year: 'numeric'
                        })}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {new Date(redaction.redacted_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { 
                          hour: '2-digit', 
                          minute: '2-digit'
                        })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    {getStatusBadge(redaction.review_status)}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    {redaction.review_notes ? (
                      <span className="text-slate-900 line-clamp-2 max-w-xs">{redaction.review_notes}</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-gray-400 text-sm">—</span>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                    <DropdownMenu>
                      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm">
                          <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                      <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
                        <DropdownMenuItem 
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleViewPdf(redaction.id);
                          }}
                        >
                          <Eye className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#03667a' }} />
                          View PDF
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                        <DropdownMenuItem 
                          onClick={async (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            try {
                              const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/pii-redaction/${redaction.id}/download`);
                              if (response.ok) {
                                const blob = await response.blob();
                                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                                const link = document.createElement('a');
                                link.href = url;
                                link.download = `redacted_${redaction.file_name}.pdf`;
                                document.body.appendChild(link);
                                link.click();
                                document.body.removeChild(link);
                                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                              } else {
                                console.error('Failed to download:', response.statusText);
                                alert('Failed to download file. Please check if the file exists in storage.');
                              }
                            } catch (error) {
                              console.error('Download failed:', error);
                              alert('Error downloading file. Please try again.');
                            }
                          }}
                        >
                          <Download className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#03667a' }} />
                          Download PDF
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                        <DropdownMenuItem
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleReviewAction(redaction.id, 'approved');
                          }}
                          disabled={actionInProgress === redaction.id}
                        >
                          <CheckCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#93d500' }} />
                          Approve
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                        <DropdownMenuItem
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            const notes = prompt('Enter rejection notes (optional):');
                            handleReviewAction(redaction.id, 'rejected', notes || undefined);
                          }}
                          disabled={actionInProgress === redaction.id}
                        >
                          <XCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#565656' }} />
                          Reject
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                        {redaction.review_status !== 'needs_review' && (
                          <DropdownMenuItem
                            onClick={(e) => {
                              e.stopPropagation();
                              handleReviewAction(redaction.id, 'needs_review');
                            }}
                            disabled={actionInProgress === redaction.id}
                          >
                            <RotateCcw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" style={{ color: '#f59e0b' }} />
                            Mark as Needs Review
                          </DropdownMenuItem>
                        )}
                        <DropdownMenuItem
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleDelete(redaction.id, redaction.file_name);
                          }}
                          disabled={actionInProgress === redaction.id}
                          className="text-red-600 focus:text-red-600"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                          Delete
                        </DropdownMenuItem>
                      </DropdownMenuContent>
                    </DropdownMenu>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>

      {/* PDF Viewer Modal */}
      {pdfViewerOpen && pdfViewerUrl && (
        <PdfViewerModal
          pdfUrl={pdfViewerUrl}
          title="Redacted PDF Preview"
          onClose={() => {
            setPdfViewerOpen(false);
            setPdfViewerUrl("");
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend\context\AnalysisContext.tsx =====
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useState, ReactNode } from 'react';

interface ProgressUpdate {
  status: string;
  file: string;
  progress: number;
}

interface RedactionMetadata {
  entities_found: number;
  pages_processed: number;
  entities: Array<{
    type: string;
    value: string;
    replacement: string;
  }>;
  processing_time: number;
}

interface PDFPage {
  pageNumber: number;
  originalImage: string;
  redactedImage: string;
}

interface RedactionResult {
  fileName: string;
  metadata: RedactionMetadata;
  redactedFileUrl: string;
  pdfPages: PDFPage[];
}

interface AnalysisContextType {
  // Progress tracking
  currentPage: number;
  setCurrentPage: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>;
  progressByFile: Record<string, ProgressUpdate[]>;
  setProgressByFile: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, ProgressUpdate[]>>>;
  
  // PII Redaction state
  piiRedactionResults: RedactionResult[];
  setPiiRedactionResults: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<RedactionResult[]>>;
  piiCurrentPage: number;
  setPiiCurrentPage: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>;
  piiZoom: number;
  setPiiZoom: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>;
}

const AnalysisContext = createContext<AnalysisContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AnalysisProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
  const [progressByFile, setProgressByFile] = useState<Record<string, ProgressUpdate[]>>({});
  
  // PII Redaction state
  const [piiRedactionResults, setPiiRedactionResults] = useState<RedactionResult[]>([]);
  const [piiCurrentPage, setPiiCurrentPage] = useState(0);
  const [piiZoom, setPiiZoom] = useState(1.0);

  return (
    <AnalysisContext.Provider
      value={{
        currentPage,
        setCurrentPage,
        progressByFile,
        setProgressByFile,
        piiRedactionResults,
        setPiiRedactionResults,
        piiCurrentPage,
        setPiiCurrentPage,
        piiZoom,
        setPiiZoom,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AnalysisContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAnalysisContext() {
  const context = useContext(AnalysisContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useAnalysisContext must be used within an AnalysisProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

/**
 * API Configuration
 * Centralized API URL configuration for all frontend components
 * Uses empty string for relative paths (proxied by Next.js in development)
 */
export const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || '';

===== frontend\lib\useProgressPolling.ts =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { API_URL } from './api-config';

interface ProgressUpdate {
  status: string;
  file: string;
  progress: number;
  timestamp?: string;
}

interface ProgressResponse {
  status: 'processing' | 'completed' | 'error';
  files: {
    [filename: string]: ProgressUpdate[];
  };
  completed_count: number;
  total_count: number;
}

export function useProgressPolling(sessionId: string | null, isActive: boolean) {
  const [progressByFile, setProgressByFile] = useState<{ [key: string]: ProgressUpdate[] }>({});
  const [isPolling, setIsPolling] = useState(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    console.log(`🔄 useProgressPolling effect - sessionId: ${sessionId}, isActive: ${isActive}`);
    
    if (!sessionId || !isActive) {
      console.log(`⏹️ Stopping polling - sessionId: ${sessionId}, isActive: ${isActive}`);
      if (intervalRef.current) {
        clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
        intervalRef.current = null;
      }
      setIsPolling(false);
      return;
    }

    console.log(`▶️ Starting polling for session: ${sessionId}`);
    setIsPolling(true);

    const pollProgress = async () => {
      try {
        console.log(`📊 [${new Date().toISOString()}] Fetching progress for session: ${sessionId}`);
        
        console.log(`📊 Fetching from: ${API_URL}/api/progress/${sessionId}`);
        
        const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/progress/${sessionId}`, {
          cache: 'no-store',
          mode: 'cors',
          credentials: 'include',
        });
        
        console.log(`📊 Response received! Status: ${response.status}`);
        
        if (response.ok) {
          const text = await response.text();
          console.log(`📊 Raw response (${text.length} chars):`, text.substring(0, 200));
          
          const data: ProgressResponse = JSON.parse(text);
          console.log(`📊 ✅ PARSED DATA:`, data);
          console.log(`📊 Files object:`, data.files);
          console.log(`📊 Files keys:`, Object.keys(data.files || {}));
          
          if (Object.keys(data.files || {}).length > 0) {
            console.log(`📊 🎯 SETTING STATE with ${Object.keys(data.files).length} files`);
            setProgressByFile(data.files);
            console.log(`✅ setProgressByFile() called`);
          } else {
            console.log(`⚠️ No files in response yet`);
          }

          // Stop polling if completed or error
          if (data.status === 'completed' || data.status === 'error') {
            console.log(`✅ Analysis ${data.status}, stopping polling`);
            if (intervalRef.current) {
              clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
              intervalRef.current = null;
            }
            setIsPolling(false);
          }
        } else {
          const errorText = await response.text();
          console.error(`❌ Progress polling failed - Status: ${response.status}, Body:`, errorText);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError') {
          console.error(`❌ [${new Date().toISOString()}] Fetch timeout after 5 seconds`);
        } else {
          console.error(`❌ [${new Date().toISOString()}] Error polling progress:`, error);
        }
      }
    };

    // Delay initial poll by 500ms to allow backend to initialize session
    console.log(`🚀 Scheduling initial poll in 500ms`);
    const initialTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      console.log(`🚀 Running initial poll now`);
      pollProgress();
    }, 500);

    // Poll every 2 seconds
    console.log(`⏰ Setting up 2-second interval`);
    intervalRef.current = setInterval(pollProgress, 2000);

    return () => {
      console.log(`🧹 Cleaning up polling interval and timeout`);
      if (intervalRef.current) {
        clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
        intervalRef.current = null;
      }
      clearTimeout(initialTimeout);
    };
  }, [sessionId, isActive]);

  const clearProgress = () => {
    setProgressByFile({});
  };

  return {
    progressByFile,
    isPolling,
    clearProgress,
  };
}

}

===== frontend\components\ui\badge.tsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-transparent bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/80",
        secondary:
          "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-secondary/80",
        destructive:
          "border-transparent bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow hover:bg-destructive/80",
        outline: "text-foreground",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

export interface BadgeProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {}

function Badge({ className, variant, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <div className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />
  )
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

===== frontend\components\ui\button.tsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-background shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
    return (
      <Comp
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
        ref={ref}
        {...props}
      />
    )
  }
)
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { Button, buttonVariants }

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as DialogPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-dialog"
import { X } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const Dialog = DialogPrimitive.Root

const DialogTrigger = DialogPrimitive.Trigger

const DialogPortal = DialogPrimitive.Portal

const DialogClose = DialogPrimitive.Close

const DialogOverlay = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80  data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
))
DialogOverlay.displayName = DialogPrimitive.Overlay.displayName

const DialogContent = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <DialogPortal>
    <DialogOverlay />
    <DialogPrimitive.Content
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "fixed left-[50%] top-[50%] z-50 grid w-full max-w-lg translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] gap-4 border bg-background p-6 shadow-lg duration-200 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%] sm:rounded-lg",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <DialogPrimitive.Close className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-sm opacity-70 ring-offset-background transition-opacity hover:opacity-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none data-[state=open]:bg-accent data-[state=open]:text-muted-foreground">
        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
        <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
      </DialogPrimitive.Close>
    </DialogPrimitive.Content>
  </DialogPortal>
))
DialogContent.displayName = DialogPrimitive.Content.displayName

const DialogHeader = ({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
  <div
    className={cn(
      "flex flex-col space-y-1.5 text-center sm:text-left",

    )}

  />
)
DialogHeader.displayName = "DialogHeader"

  ...props

DialogFooter.displayName = "DialogFooter"
